The user wants a "Sokoban Pac-Man game" — a fusion of Sokoban (pushing boxes) and Pac-Man (maze, pellets, ghosts). Let me think about how to combine them:

- Player is the Pac-Man character inside a Sokoban maze
- Push boxes/crates to target spots (Sokoban core mechanic)
- Eat pellets scattered in the maze (Pac-Man element)
- Ghosts roam the maze and chase you (Pac-Man element) — but wait, if ghosts can enter corridors that are one tile wide and Sokoban has push-only mechanics... ghosts can move freely through open tiles. If a ghost catches Pac-Man, you lose a life or game over.

Design:
- Grid-based maze. Walls, floors, pellets, boxes (crates), targets (docks).
- Player moves with arrow keys/WASD. Pushing a box into another box or a wall = blocked.
- When all boxes are on targets → level clear. Pellets give score; eating all pellets can also contribute to victory or just bonus. Ghost AI: simple chase (BFS toward player, or greedy). If ghost reaches the player's tile → lose a life / game over with restart.

To make it fair, ghosts should move slower (e.g., every 2 ticks, or when the player moves), and there might be "power pellets" that temporarily turn ghosts into frightened state (fleeing) — you can eat them for points! This is a great fusion: power pellets make ghosts flee; if you touch a frightened ghost, you eat it.

Level design: I need to carefully craft Sokoban levels that are solvable while also being interesting enough for Pac-Man. Must ensure solvability. Let me design a smallish level (e.g., 13x11 or 15x11).

Let me carefully design the levels. Symbols:
- `#` wall
- `.` floor
- `$` box
- `@` player
- `*` box on target
- `+` target (dot? no) — use `%` for target, `o` for pellet, `O` for power pellet.

Strategy: keep level 1 very open (few wall stubs), place boxes so they can be freely pushed around in a large open area with targets as simple pockets that aren't corners — or even flat floors. Now the game mechanics:

Core loop:
- Grid rendering on canvas, crisp tiles with scale to fit window.
- Player: draw a Pac-Man-style character — yellow circle with animated mouth, facing movement direction.
- Boxes: wooden crates (Sokoban) — call them "crates". Draw with wood grain lines + shading.
- Targets: glowing pads/docks (like charging docks), pulsing. When box is on target → lit up (glowing green/checkmark).
- Pellets: small dots; power pellets are larger and blink.
- Ghosts: classic Blinky/Pinky-style ghosts with eyes that look toward the player, wavy bottom edges, bobbing animation. 1-2 per level.
- Ghost AI: BFS pathfinding to player every ghost step (or greedy). If frightened (after power pellet), flee (BFS away from player / random). If touched while frightened → eaten (score+, respawn at start position with brief invulnerability, or removed for a few seconds then come back — classic: all ghosts return after being eaten; simpler: eaten ghost respawns at ghost house after 3s).
- Collision: if ghost shares tile with player (or swaps tiles), and not frightened → lose a life. Lives = 3. Lose all → game over screen with restart.
- Level clear: all boxes on targets → level complete overlay, then next level; after final level → victory screen + score, restartable. Pellets: eating all is optional bonus? Let me make pellets just scoring (eat for points), and power pellets trigger frightened state. Also add "extra life at 5000" maybe — nice touch but optional; keep it simple: no extra life, or yes at a threshold. Let me add +1 life at 3000 with a floating text. Hmm, scope. Maybe include it — it's cheap.

Scoring: pellet 10, power 50, box on target 100 (and if you push a box OFF target -? no, just track), box off target = -25? Keep it simple: +100 per box placed (only when newly placed; remove from target doesn't deduct... actually classic sokoban doesn't score like that; but for game feel: award 100 on placement, small penalty on removal to prevent spam? Let me do: +100 on placement, -25 on removal (floor at 0)? Simpler: only count "moves" and boxes placed. Award 100 each time a box lands on target (no deduction) — you can cheese it a bit but it's casual. Fine.) Ghost eat: 200.

Movement: grid step with smooth animation interpolation for player/ghosts/boxes. Input queue: buffer next direction; hold key to repeat move. Also support arrow keys + WASD, P to pause, R to restart level? Spec says "way to restart without reloading" — game over screen restart button + keyboard (Enter/Space). Let me also add a Restart button in the HUD (resets current run) and M for mute.

Ghost movement timing: ghosts move every N milliseconds (e.g., 450ms L1, 420 L2, 380 L3), independent of player, using BFS toward the player's tile (recompute each step). Frightened state: duration based on level (e.g., 6s), ghosts turn blue/purple, move slower (×1.5 interval) and flee (BFS to furthest tile from player, or away = pick a neighbor maximizing distance to player; simpler: BFS from player, ghost moves to adjacent tile with max distance — but that can oscillate; add randomness among top options). Frightened ghosts also avoid reversing unless dead-end.

BFS pathfinding: recompute each ghost step from ghost to player (target = player's tile). Grid is small (≤19x11=209 cells) — BFS trivially cheap. Frightened: instead compute distance field from player once per ghost step, and move to adjacent floor tile with max distance (with tie-break random, avoid reversing if possible). Ghosts can't enter walls; can they pass through boxes? In Pac-Man ghosts ignore walls... but in Sokoban, boxes are obstacles. Ghosts should treat boxes as walls (they can't push — or should they?! Fun idea: ghosts CAN push boxes! That's a chaotic and fun fusion element... but it could break solvability (ghost pushes your box off target). Hmm — that's actually a great risk/reward mechanic for the "Sokoban Pac-Man" identity! But it might frustrate. Let me make ghosts NOT pass through or push boxes: boxes are solid to ghosts. This keeps Sokoban integrity. But if a box creates a wall, BFS handles it (unreachable → ghost wanders randomly). Edge case: player encloses ghost in a pocket with boxes → ghost can't reach; that's fine (player's win by design). Also if the player is unreachable from the ghost due to boxes, the ghost picks a random neighbor.

Ghost step logic detail:
- Every ghostInterval ms (per level; frightened ghosts use interval ×1.6), for each non-eaten, non-dizzy ghost: compute move. Target = player tile. BFS over floor tiles (walls and boxes are blocked; target tile = player's current tile which is always walkable). Choose neighbor that minimizes distance to target; tie-break: prefer not reversing (unless dead-end), random among ties. Dijkstra-like with a slight preference for straight? Keep it simple: dist map from player; pick min-distance neighbor; tie → random but avoid reverse if alternative exists.
- Frightened: use same dist map but pick MAX distance neighbor, avoid reverse unless forced; also 25% chance to pick a random neighbor (flustered). If all neighbors are walls → stay.
- Ghost animation: interpolate from previous tile to new tile over ghostInterval — store t progress each frame.

Catch check timing: after player completes step, and after each ghost completes step: if same tile → if frightened → eat; else → death. Also swap detection during player step (ghost moves on its own clock so exact simultaneous is rare, but check segment intersection at the moment of either arrival). Simplest robust: every frame, if a live non-dizzy ghost's current logical tile == player's logical tile and player not invuln → resolve. Since both snap to tiles at completion, mid-move overlap only occurs on swap; let me also do a midpoint check: if player's moving segment and ghost's moving segment share an endpoint (P1==G0 or G1==P0) → resolve at that moment. Let me implement: after player step completes: for each ghost, if ghost.tile == player.tile OR (ghost was on player's new tile just before its own move...) — too fiddly. Pragmatic: check every frame using interpolated positions: if dist(playerPos, ghostPos) < 0.55 tile → resolve. This handles swaps naturally (they pass through each other). Use logical animation position (lerp).

Dizzy/invuln: on death: lives--, if lives<=0 → game over; else reset player to start, ghosts to spawn, all ghosts dizzy 3s, player invuln blink 2s, brief "OUCH!" flash + shake. During invuln, ghost overlap does nothing (player blinks).

BFS implementation:
```
function bfsField(sx,sy){ // returns Map key->dist from (sx,sy) over walkable cells
  const q=[[sx,sy]]; const dist=new Map(); dist.set(k(sx,sy),0);
  while(q.length){ const [x,y]=q.shift(); ... } 
}
```
Grid ≤ 209 cells, fine. Use array-based queue with index pointer for speed.

Walkable: not wall && not box (box positions set rebuilt per ghost step — cheap). Ghosts also can't pass... player? Player tile is walkable (that's the target). OK.

Ghost step selection:
```
function ghostNext(g){
  const opts = dirs.filter(d => walkable(g.x+d.x, g.y+d.y));
  if(!opts.length) return null;
  let target = g.brain(player); // {x,y} or null
  let best=null,bd=Infinity;
  if(target!=null){
    const field = bfsField(target.x,target.y); // distance from target to each cell
    for(const d of opts){ const key=k(g.x+d.x,g.y+d.y); const dd=field.has(key)?field.get(key):Infinity;
      // avoid reverse: penalty
      const rev = (d.x===-g.lastDx && d.y===-g.lastDy) ? 0.5 : 0;
      if(dd+rev < bd - 1e-9){bd=dd+rev;best=d;} else if(Math.abs(dd+rev-bd)<1e-9 && Math.random()<0.35){best=d;}
    }
  } else {
    const nonRev = opts.filter(d=>!(d.x===-g.lastDx&&d.y===-g.lastDy));
    best=(nonRev.length?nonRev:opts)[rand];
  }
  // frightened: invert — pick max dd with small random; also 25% full random
}
```
For frightened, compute field from player (distance from player), and pick neighbor maximizing dd.

To avoid BFS per ghost per step cost duplication (2 ghosts → 2 BFS/step ~ trivial).

If box at (nx,ny): bx=nx+dx... check beyond not wall/box → push: animate box (box.px,py = old; x,y=new), player moves; else blocked. Else move. Set player.px,py=old, x,y=new, animT=0; on arrival (animT reaches 1) process tile pickup + placement check for pushed box + level clear check.

Now JS. Let me write it in a clean IIFE-ish style. Key parts:

```js
const LEVELS=[ {name:'DOCK ONE', rows:[...], ghosts:[{x:12,y:8,color:'#ff5a6e',speed:500,brain:'chase'}], fright:7, par:90}, ...];
```

State vars: levelIdx, grid {cols,rows,walls:Set,targets:Set,pellets:Set(powers separate? mark 'o'/'O' — store pellets as Map key->'dot'|'power'), boxes:[{x,y,px,py,t,on}], player:{...}, ghosts:[...], score,best,lives,chain,dotsEaten,dotsTotal,state,stateT,...

Parse:
```js
function loadLevel(i){ const L=LEVELS[i]; rows=L.rows; cols=max len; 
 walls=new Set(); targets=new Set(); pellets=new Map(); boxes=[]; 
 for y..for x..c=rows[y][x]:
  '#':walls.add(k)
  '.': nothing
  'o':pellets.set(k,'dot')
  'O':pellets.set(k,'power')
  '%':targets.add(k)
  '$':boxes.push({x,y,px:x,py:y,t:1}); (also floor)
  '@':player start
  '*' not used.
 ghost objects from L.ghosts with reset fields.
 dotsTotal=pellets.size; dotsEaten=0; chain=0; frightT=0;
 buildStaticLayer(); updateHUD(); state='ready'; stateT=0;
}
```

boxAt(x,y): boxes.find(b=>b.x===x&&b.y===y). walkable(x,y)= !walls.has && in-bounds. boxBlock for ghosts: set of box keys rebuilt each ghost step.

Player step attempt:
```js
function tryMove(d){ // d {x,y}
 if(player.t<1) return;
 const nx=player.x+d.x, ny=player.y+d.y;
 if(walls.has(k(nx,ny))) { sfxBlocked? nudge } 
 const b=boxAt(nx,ny);
 if(b){ const bx=nx+d.x, by=ny+d.y;
   if(walls.has(k(bx,by))||boxAt(bx,by)) return;
   b.px=b.x;b.py=b.y;b.x=bx;b.y=by;b.t=0; playerMoved(d); onPush();
 } else playerMoved(d);
}
function playerMoved(d){ player.px=player.x;player.py=player.y; player.x+=d.x;player.y+=d.y; player.t=0; player.face=d; }
```
On update: player.t += dt/130ms; when t>=1 && was<1 → arrival: eat pellet at tile (dot sfx, power → startFright), if a box just arrived on this move? Box has its own t; when box.t crosses 1 check target placement: if targets.has(k) && !b.wasOnTarget → place FX. Track b.on = targets.has(key). Compute on arrival: if now on && !wasOn → score+100, sfx, particles, floater; if wasOn && !now → small blip (no penalty). 

Level clear check after box arrival: all targets covered → beginClear().

beginClear(): state='clear'; stateT=0; bonus = Math.max(0, L.par - Math.floor(levelTime))*10; score+=bonus; save? show overlay with stats; sfxClear. After 2.4s or Enter → next.

Ghost update:
```js
ghostTimer[g.id] += dt; interval = g.speed * (frightT>0?1.7:1) * (g.kind==='wisp'?1.15:1);
while(g.timer>=interval && state==='play'){ g.timer-=interval; ghostStep(g); }
```
Actually keep per-ghost accumulator; reset on respawn.

ghostStep(g): 
```js
const opts=DIRS.filter(d=>walkG(g.x+d.x,g.y+d.y));
if(!opts.length) return;
let pick;
const fright = frightT>0 && !g.eaten && g.dizzyUntil<now? ... (dizzy ghosts wander slowly?) let me have dizzy ghosts not move at all (frozen, blinking). Simpler and fair.
if(frightT>0){
  if(Math.random()<0.3) pick=rand(opts);
  else{ const field=bfsField(player.x,player.y); // distance from player
    best = argmax(field.get(neighbor), tie random, avoid reverse unless only)
  }
} else {
  const target = g.brain();
  if(!target){ wander }
  else { field=bfsField(target...); pick neighbor min dist (avoid reverse) }
}
g.px=g.x;g.py=g.y; g.x+=pick.x;g.y+=pick.y; g.t=0; g.lastD=pick;
```
Wait, bfsField from target gives distance from target to each cell — the ghost wants to minimize its own distance to target: field computed with BFS starting at target → field[ghostNeighbor] = distance. ✓

brains:
- chase: return {x:player.x,y:player.y}
- ambush: return tile 2 ahead of player facing (clamp/wall check? if wall, fall back to player)
- wisp: Math.random()<0.6 ? player : null (wander)

Collision each frame (state play): for each ghost !eaten && dizzyUntil<now: 
```js
const gp=lerpPos(g), pp=lerpPos(player);
if(invuln) skip;
if(frightT>0){ if(dist<0.55*tile? use tile units <0.6) eatGhost(g); }
else if(dist<0.6) die();
```
Positions in tile units for computation.

eatGhost(g): g.eaten=true; g.eatT=0 (3s timer then respawn at spawn point, dizzy 2s); score+=200*chain; chain*=2; floater; sfx; particles poof. If ghost was... fine.

die(): state='dying'; stateT=0; sfxDeath; shake; particles burst of yellow shards at player pos; after 1.1s: lives--; if lives<=0 {state='over'; show overlay; save best} else { resetPositions(); player.invulnUntil=now+2000; ghosts dizzy now+3000; state='ready'; stateT=0 (shorter, 0.9s)}

resetPositions: player to start (t=1), boxes stay!, ghosts to spawn with t=1 timer=0. Note: if ghost spawn tile is now occupied by a box → BFS handles unreachable (wander). Could a box land on the spawn? L1 spawn (12,8) — could a box be pushed there? Boxes can be anywhere... possible! Then ghost stuck wandering forever in a pocket. Acceptable edge case (player's doing); ghost just wanders where it can. Fine.

Fright: frightT>0 counts down; on start: chain=1; sfxPower; ghosts flash. On end: chain=0.

Extra life: after score add, if !extraGiven && score>=5000 {lives++; extraGiven=true; sfx; floater "EXTRA LIFE!"}.

Now code for bfsField(sx,sy):
```js
function bfsField(sx,sy){
  const dist=new Int16Array(cols*rows).fill(-1); // rows*cols max 209 fine
  const q=[]; let head=0;
  const sIdx=sy*cols+sx; dist[sIdx]=0; q.push(sIdx);
  while(head<q.length){
    const ci=q[head++]; const cx=ci%cols, cy=(ci-cx)/cols; const cd=dist[ci];
    for(const d of DIRS){ const nx=cx+d.x, ny=cy+d.y; if(nx<0||ny<0||nx>=cols||ny>=rows) continue;
      const ni=ny*cols+nx; if(dist[ni]!==-1) continue;
      if(walls.has(ni) || boxSet.has(ni)) continue;
      dist[ni]=cd+1; q.push(ni); }
  }
  return {dist, get:(x,y)=> (y>=0&&x>=0&&x<cols&&y<rows)?dist[y*cols+x]:-1};
}
```
Use numeric keys for walls/boxes/targets/pellets: key = y*cols+x. Level parsing needs cols known first (max row length). Let me store sets of numbers.

Ghost step:
```js
function ghostStep(g, now){
  const opts=[];
  for(const d of DIRS){ const nx=g.x+d.x, ny=g.y+d.y;
    if(nx<0||ny<0||nx>=cols||ny>=rows) continue; if(walls.has(ny*cols+nx)) continue; if(boxSet.has(ny*cols+nx)) continue; opts.push(d);} 
  if(!opts.length) return;
  let pick=null;
  const fright = frightT>0;
  if(fright){
    if(Math.random()<0.35){ pick=opts[(Math.random()*opts.length)|0]; }
    else{
      const f=bfsField(player.x,player.y);
      let bd=-1,bt=[];
      for(const d of opts){ const dd=f.get(g.x+d.x,g.y+d.y); if(dd<0) continue;
        const rev=(d.x===-g.lx&&d.y===-g.ly)?0.25:0;
        const s=dd-rev;
        if(s>bd+1e-9){bd=s;bt=[d];} else if(Math.abs(s-bd)<1e-9) bt.push(d); }
      pick=bt.length?bt[(Math.random()*bt.length)|0]:opts[(Math.random()*opts.length)|0];
    }
  } else {
    const tgt = brainTarget(g);
    if(tgt && (tgt.x!==g.x||tgt.y!==g.y)){
      const f=bfsField(tgt.x,tgt.y);
      let bd=Infinity,bt=[];
      for(const d of opts){ const dd=f.get(g.x+d.x,g.y+d.y); if(dd<0) continue;
        const rev=(d.x===-g.lx&&d.y===-g.ly)?0.25:0;
        const s=dd+rev;
        if(s<bd-1e-9){bd=s;bt=[d];} else if(Math.abs(s-bd)<1e-9) bt.push(d);} 
      pick=bt.length?bt[(Math.random()*bt.length)|0]:null;
    }
    if(!pick){ const nr=opts.filter(d=>!(d.x===-g.lx&&d.y===-g.ly)); pick=(nr.length?nr:opts)[(Math.random()*(nr.length?nr.length:opts.length))|0]; }
  }
  g.px=g.x; g.py=g.y; g.x+=pick.x; g.y+=pick.y; g.lx=pick.x; g.ly=pick.y; g.t=0;
}
```
brainTarget: chase → player; ambush → 2 ahead of player facing (if that tile is wall, use 1 ahead or player); wisp → Math.random()<0.55 ? player : null.

Note: ghost interval while frightened = speed*1.7.

```js
let AC=null, master=null, muted=false;
function audio(){ if(!AC){ try{ AC=new (window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext)(); master=AC.createGain(); master.gain.value=0.16; master.connect(AC.destination);}catch(e){} } if(AC&&AC.state==='suspended') AC.resume(); return AC; }
function tone(f0,f1,dur,type,vol,delay=0){ if(!AC||muted) return; const t=AC.currentTime+delay; const o=AC.createOscillator(), g=AC.createGain(); o.type=type; o.frequency.setValueAtTime(f0,t); if(f1&&f1!==f0) o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(1,f1),t+dur); g.gain.setValueAtTime(vol,t); g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001,t+dur); o.connect(g); g.connect(master); o.start(t); o.stop(t+dur+0.02); }
```

Now code for bfsField(sx,sy):
```js
function bfsField(sx,sy){
  const dist=new Int16Array(cols*rows).fill(-1); // rows*cols max 209 fine
  const q=[]; let head=0;
  const sIdx=sy*cols+sx; dist[sIdx]=0; q.push(sIdx);
  while(head<q.length){
    const ci=q[head++]; const cx=ci%cols, cy=(ci-cx)/cols; const cd=dist[ci];
    for(const d of DIRS){ const nx=cx+d.x, ny=cy+d.y; if(nx<0||ny<0||nx>=cols||ny>=rows) continue;
      const ni=ny*cols+nx; if(dist[ni]!==-1) continue;
      if(walls.has(ni) || boxSet.has(ni)) continue;
      dist[ni]=cd+1; q.push(ni); }
  }
  return {dist, get:(x,y)=> (y>=0&&x>=0&&x<cols&&y<rows)?dist[y*cols+x]:-1};
}
```
Use numeric keys for walls/boxes/targets/pellets: key = y*cols+x. Level parsing needs cols known first (max row length). Let me store sets of numbers.

Ghost step:
```js
function ghostStep(g, now){
  const opts=[];
  for(const d of DIRS){ const nx=g.x+d.x, ny=g.y+d.y;
    if(nx<0||ny<0||nx>=cols||ny>=rows) continue; if(walls.has(ny*cols+nx)) continue; if(boxSet.has(ny*cols+nx)) continue; opts.push(d);} 
  if(!opts.length) return;
  let pick=null;
  const fright = frightT>0;
  if(fright){
    if(Math.random()<0.35){ pick=opts[(Math.random()*opts.length)|0]; }
    else{
      const f=bfsField(player.x,player.y);
      let bd=-1,bt=[];
      for(const d of opts){ const dd=f.get(g.x+d.x,g.y+d.y); if(dd<0) continue;
        const rev=(d.x===-g.lx&&d.y===-g.ly)?0.25:0;
        const s=dd-rev;
        if(s>bd+1e-9){bd=s;bt=[d];} else if(Math.abs(s-bd)<1e-9) bt.push(d); }
      pick=bt.length?bt[(Math.random()*bt.length)|0]:opts[(Math.random()*opts.length)|0];
    }
  } else {
    const tgt = brainTarget(g);
    if(tgt && (tgt.x!==g.x||tgt.y!==g.y)){
      const f=bfsField(tgt.x,tgt.y);
      let bd=Infinity,bt=[];
      for(const d of opts){ const dd=f.get(g.x+d.x,g.y+d.y); if(dd<0) continue;
        const rev=(d.x===-g.lx&&d.y===-g.ly)?0.25:0;
        const s=dd+rev;
        if(s<bd-1e-9){bd=s;bt=[d];} else if(Math.abs(s-bd)<1e-9) bt.push(d);} 
      pick=bt.length?bt[(Math.random()*bt.length)|0]:null;
    }
    if(!pick){ const nr=opts.filter(d=>!(d.x===-g.lx&&d.y===-g.ly)); pick=(nr.length?nr:opts)[(Math.random()*(nr.length?nr.length:opts.length))|0]; }
  }
  g.px=g.x; g.py=g.y; g.x+=pick.x; g.y+=pick.y; g.lx=pick.x; g.ly=pick.y; g.t=0;
}
```
brainTarget: chase → player; ambush → 2 ahead of player facing (if that tile is wall, use 1 ahead or player); wisp → Math.random()<0.55 ? player : null.

Note: ghost interval while frightened = speed*1.7.

Ghost animation t progresses at dt/interval — need per-ghost current interval; store g.t += dt/g.curInt where curInt set when step happens (or computed each frame). Simpler: g.t += dt / (frightT>0?g.speed*1.7:g.speed). When t>=1 → step & reset t-=1 (carry remainder).

Collision uses lerp position: pos = px+(x-px)*easeOutQuad(t)? For collision use linear t (no easing) to keep it consistent — or small tolerance 0.6 tile with ease fine. Let me use smoothstep? Keep linear for both (movement is fast, looks fine).

Player movement timing: STEP=135ms.

Now the update loop:

Sound implementation:

```js
let AC=null, master=null, muted=false;
function audio(){ if(!AC){ try{ AC=new (window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext)(); master=AC.createGain(); master.gain.value=0.16; master.connect(AC.destination);}catch(e){} } if(AC&&AC.state==='suspended') AC.resume(); return AC; }
function tone(f0,f1,dur,type,vol,delay=0){ if(!AC||muted) return; const t=AC.currentTime+delay; const o=AC.createOscillator(), g=AC.createGain(); o.type=type; o.frequency.setValueAtTime(f0,t); if(f1&&f1!==f0) o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(1,f1),t+dur); g.gain.setValueAtTime(vol,t); g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001,t+dur); o.connect(g); g.connect(master); o.start(t); o.stop(t+dur+0.02); }
sfx = { dot:()=>{tone(dotFlip?740:620, dotFlip?900:780, .06,'square',.5); dotFlip=!dotFlip;}, ... }
```

Key global state variables:
```js
let levelIdx=0, cols=0, rowsN=0;

let player={x:1,y:1,px:1,py:1,t:1,lx:1,ly:0,face:{x:1,y:0},invulnUntil:0};
let ghosts=[];
let score=0,best=0,lives=3,chain=0,frightT=0,extraGiven=false;
let state='menu',stateT=0,levelTime=0,dotsEaten=0,dotsTotal=0;
```

Parsing:
```js
function loadLevel(i){
  levelIdx=i; const L=LEVELS[i];
  rowsN=L.rows.length; cols=Math.max(...L.rows.map(r=>r.length));
  walls=new Set(); targets=new Set(); pellets=new Map(); boxes=[];
  for(let y=0;y<rowsN;y++){ const row=L.rows[y];
    for(let x=0;x<cols;x++){ const c=x<row.length?row[x]:'#'; const k=y*cols+x;
      if(c==='#') walls.add(k);
      else if(c==='o') pellets.set(k,'dot');
      else if(c==='O') pellets.set(k,'power');
      else if(c==='%') targets.add(k);
      else if(c==='$'){ boxes.push({x,y,px:x,py:y,t:1,on:false,pop:0}); }
      else if(c==='@'){ player.x=x;player.y=y;player.px=x;player.py=y;player.t=1; }
    } }
  boxSet=new Set(boxes.map(b=>b.y*cols+b.x));
  boxes.forEach(b=>b.on=targets.has(b.y*cols+b.x));
  ghosts=L.ghosts.map((g,j)=>({x:g.x,y:g.y,px:g.x,py:g.y,t:1,lx:0,ly:-1,color:g.color,speed:g.speed,kind:g.kind,eaten:false,eatT:0,dizzyUntil:0,acc:Math.random()*g.speed,id:j}));
  frightT=0;chain=0;levelTime=0;dotsEaten=0;dotsTotal=pellets.size;
  particles=[];floaters=[];shake=0;buffer=null;held.length=0;
  player.invulnUntil=0;player.t=1;
  updateHUD(); buildStatic();
}
```

startRun(): score=0;lives=3;extraGiven=false;runDots=0;runGhosts=0; loadLevel(0); state='ready';stateT=0; showScreen(null); sfx.ui();
restartLevel(): const keepLives=lives; loadLevel(levelIdx); state='ready';stateT=0; (score preserved) — also clear screens.
nextLevel(): if levelIdx+1<LEVELS.length { loadLevel(levelIdx+1); state='ready'; } else { winGame(); }
winGame(): state='win'; saveBest(); fill win stats; showScreen('win'); sfx.clear(); big particles.
gameOver(): state='over'; saveBest(); fill over stats; showScreen('over').

saveBest(): if(score>best){best=score; try{localStorage.setItem('pacoban.best',String(best));}catch(e){}} updateHUD().

addScore(n, x,y,color?): score+=n; if(!extraGiven&&score>=5000){extraGiven=true;lives++; sfx.extra(); floaters.push(EXTRA LIFE at center)} updateHUD(); if(score>best) best=score (live update best display but save only on end? Save whenever — harmless: wrap in try/catch each time? Let me just save on death/win to be safe.)

Player step completion handler:
```js
function playerArrive(){
  const k=player.y*cols+player.x;
  if(pellets.has(k)){ const p=pellets.get(k); pellets.delete(k); dotsEaten++; runDots++;
    if(p==='dot'){ addScore(10); sfx.dot(); sparkDot(player...)} 
    else { addScore(50); chain=1; frightT=LEVELS[levelIdx].fright*1000; sfx.power(); ring fx; floaters "FEAST!" }
    updateHUD();
  }
}
```
Box arrival:
```js
function boxArrive(b){
  const k=b.y*cols+b.x; const on=targets.has(k);
  if(on&&!b.on){ b.pop=1; addScore(100, at pos, '+100'); sfx.place(); ring+spark green; shake=Math.max(shake,.25);} 
  else if(!on&&b.on){ sfx.unplace(); }
  b.on=on;
  if(targetsCovered()) beginClear();
}
function targetsCovered(){ for(const k of targets){ const b=boxes.find(b=>b.y*cols+b.x===k); if(!b) return false;} return true; }
```
Better: maintain count onTarget; update on each boxArrive. Since boxes only change via push, recompute cheaply (targets ≤4): fine.

beginClear(): state='clear';stateT=0; const secs=Math.floor(levelTime/1000); const bonus=Math.max(0,LEVELS[levelIdx].par-secs)*10; addScore(bonus); sfx.clear(); fill #clearStats: `TIME ${secs}s · BONUS +${bonus} · DOTS ${dotsEaten}/${dotsTotal}`; showScreen('clear');

update(dt):
```js
switch(state){
 case 'menu': updateGhosts(dt,true /*wander*/); player.t=Math.min(1,player.t+dt/STEP); break;
 case 'ready': stateT+=dt; if(stateT>=READY_MS) {state='play';} break;
 case 'play': levelTime+=dt;
   if(frightT>0){ frightT-=dt; if(frightT<=0){frightT=0;chain=0;} }
   // player
   if(player.t<1) player.t=Math.min(1,player.t+dt/STEP);
   if(player.t>=1){ const d=pickDir(); if(d) tryMove(d); else ... }
   wait: player.t progresses even without input; arrival event fires once when crossing 1. Track prevT.
   // ghosts
   updateGhosts(dt,false);
   checkCollisions();
   break;
 case 'dying': stateT+=dt; if(stateT>=1100){ lives--; if(lives<=0) gameOver(); else { resetPositions(); state='ready'; stateT=0; showScreen(null);} } break;
 case 'clear': stateT+=dt; if(stateT>=2600) nextLevel(); break;
}
particles update always; floaters update; shake decay.
```

Player arrival event: manage with flag: in 'play' before progress: const was=player.t; player.t=min(1, t+dt/STEP); if(was<1&&player.t>=1){ playerArrive(); }
tryMove only attempted when fully arrived (t>=1) — but same frame as arrival OK.

```js
if(buffer && now-buffer.t<400){ const d=buffer.d; buffer=null; return d;}
return held.length?held[held.length-1]:null;
```
now = performance.now() (use ts).

tryMove(d):
```js
player.face=d;
const nx=player.x+d.x, ny=player.y+d.y, nk=ny*cols+nx;
if(nk<0||nk>=cols*rowsN) return; // out of bounds treat as wall (shouldn't happen with borders)
if(walls.has(nk)) { sfx.block(); nudge: player.nudge={d,t:0}? skip } 
const bi=boxes.findIndex(b=>b.x===nx&&b.y===ny);
if(bi>=0){ const b=boxes[bi]; const bx=nx+d.x, by=ny+d.y;
  if(walls.has(by*cols+bx) || boxes.some(o=>o!==b&&o.x===bx&&o.y===by)) { sfx.block(); return; }
  b.px=b.x;b.py=b.y;b.t=0;b.movedFromOn=b.on;
  player.px=player.x;player.py=player.y;player.x=nx;player.y=ny;player.t=0;
  sfx.push();
} else { player.px=player.x;player.py=player.y;player.x=nx;player.y=ny;player.t=0; }
```
Box t progress in update: for boxes with t<1: prev=t; t=min(1,t+dt/STEP); if crossed → boxArrive(b).

Ghost update:
```js
function updateGhosts(dt,menuMode){
 rebuildBoxSet(); // set of box keys (only needed when stepping)
 for(const g of ghosts){
   if(g.eaten){ g.eatT+=dt; if(g.eatT>=2600){ g.eaten=false; g.x=g.sx;g.y=g.sy;g.px=g.x;g.py=g.y;g.t=1;g.acc=0; g.dizzyUntil=now+2200; frightT>0? nothing } continue; }
   if(menuMode){ // wander slowly
     g.acc+=dt*0.5; const int=g.speed; if(g.t<1) g.t=Math.min(1,g.t+dt/int); 
     if(g.t>=1){ ghostStep(g,true); } continue; }
   if(now<g.dizzyUntil) { g.t=1; continue; } // frozen blinking
   const int = frightT>0? g.speed*1.7 : g.speed;
   if(g.t<1) g.t=Math.min(1, g.t+dt/int);
   if(g.t>=1){ ghostStep(g,false); g.acc=0; }
 }
}
```
Hmm the t-based stepping: when t reaches 1 we step and t resets to 0 inside ghostStep. But multiple steps per frame shouldn't happen (dt≤50ms < int). Fine.

Wait — in menuMode I want ghosts to wander at half speed → use int=g.speed*2. OK adjust.

checkCollisions():
```js
if(state!=='play') return;
const ppos=lerpP(player);
for(const g of ghosts){ if(g.eaten||now<g.dizzyUntil) continue;
  const gp=lerpP(g); const dx=gp.x-ppos.x, dy=gp.y-ppos.y;
  if(dx*dx+dy*dy < 0.62*0.62){
```

Rendering:

```js
function render(){
  ctx.setTransform(dpr,0,0,dpr,0,0);
  ctx.clearRect(0,0,stageW,stageH);
  // bg fill
  ctx.fillStyle='#05081c'; ctx.fillRect(0,0,stageW,stageH);
  if(staticCv) ctx.drawImage(staticCv,0,0,stageW,stageH);
  // shake
  const sx=shake>0.02? (Math.random()*2-1)*shake*5 :0, sy=...;
  ctx.save(); ctx.translate(sx,sy);
  drawTargets(); drawPellets(); drawBoxes(); drawGhosts(); drawPlayer(); drawParticles(); drawFloaters();
  ctx.restore();
  if(vigCv) ctx.drawImage(vigCv,0,0,stageW,stageH);
  if(state==='ready') drawReady();
}
```
Static canvas is drawn in device pixels; drawImage(staticCv, 0,0, stageW, stageH) with current transform dpr → staticCv width = stageW*dpr, so drawing at CSS size under dpr transform gives 1:1 ✓.

Body path:
```js
function ghostPath(c,x,y,r){ c.beginPath(); c.arc(x,y-r*0.12,r,Math.PI,0); const by=y+r*0.78; c.lineTo(x+r,by); const w=(2*r)/3; for(let i=0;i<3;i++){ const bx=x+r - (i+0.5)*w; c.arc(bx,by,w/2,0,Math.PI,true);} c.closePath(); }
```
Verify: arc from angle PI to 0 (default clockwise=false → counterclockwise? Canvas arc with anticlockwise=false goes from startAngle to endAngle in the direction of increasing angle. From PI to 0 increasing → PI→2PI, which is the bottom half! I want the top half: use arc(x,y,r,Math.PI,0,true)? With anticlockwise=true it goes PI→0 decreasing = through PI/2 (top) ✓. So arc(...,Math.PI,Math.PI*2,false) also gives bottom... let me be careful: angles: 0=right, PI/2=down (y down), PI=left, 3PI/2=up. Top half = from PI to 2PI through 3PI/2 (up). So arc(x,y,r,Math.PI, Math.PI*2, false) → increasing from PI to 2PI passing 3PI/2 = top ✓ starts at left point (x-r,y), ends at right point (x+r,y) ✓. Then lineTo(x+r, by) goes down the right side ✓. Skirt: from x+r to x-r with 3 upward bumps: each arc(bx, by, w/2, 0, PI, true): starts at bx+w/2 (=right end of bump), anticlockwise (decreasing angle 0→-PI? with anticlockwise=true it goes 0 → -PI through -PI/2 = up) ends at bx-w/2 ✓ upward bump. Three bumps cover x+r to x-r ✓ then closePath connects to the left side start... path started at arc start (x-r, yTopCenter?) — hmm the arc starts at angle PI which is (x-r, y-0.12r). The closePath draws a line from last point (x-r, by) back to (x-r, y-0.12r) = left side ✓.

rr helper:
```js
function rr(c,x,y,w,h,r){ c.beginPath(); c.moveTo(x+r,y); c.arcTo(x+w,y,x+w,y+h,r); c.arcTo(x+w,y+h,x,y+h,r); c.arcTo(x,y+h,x,y,r); c.arcTo(x,y,x+w,y,r); c.closePath(); }
```

```js
(function(){

const clamp=(v,a,b)=>v<a?a:v>b?b:v;
function rr(c,x,y,w,h,r){r=Math.min(r,w/2,h/2);c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x+r,y);c.arcTo(x+w,y,x+w,y+h,r);c.arcTo(x+w,y+h,x,y+h,r);c.arcTo(x,y+h,x,y,r);c.arcTo(x,y,x+w,y,r);c.closePath();}
```

```js
function makeSpr(fn){const s=document.createElement('canvas');s.width=s.height=48;fn(s.getContext('2d'));return s;}
dotSpr=makeSpr(g=>{const gr=g.createRadialGradient(24,24,1,24,24,23);gr.addColorStop(0,'#fffbe9');gr.addColorStop(.4,'#ffe9a8');gr.addColorStop(.65,'rgba(255,214,110,.35)');gr.addColorStop(1,'rgba(255,214,110,0)');g.fillStyle=gr;g.beginPath();g.arc(24,24,23,0,7);g.fill();});
powSpr=makeSpr(g=>{const gr=g.createRadialGradient(24,24,1,24,24,23);gr.addColorStop(0,'#fff');gr.addColorStop(.35,'#ffd93b');gr.addColorStop(.7,'rgba(255,176,46,.3)');gr.addColorStop(1,'rgba(255,176,46,0)');g.fillStyle=gr;g.beginPath();g.arc(24,24,23,0,7);g.fill();g.strokeStyle='rgba(255,230,150,.9)';g.lineWidth=3;g.beginPath();g.arc(24,24,15,0,7);g.stroke();});
```

Audio:
```js
let AC=null,master=null,muted=false,dotFlip=false;
function ensureAudio(){if(AC)return;try{const Ctx=window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext;if(!Ctx)return;AC=new Ctx();master=AC.createGain();master.gain.value=.16;master.connect(AC.destination);}catch(e){}}
function tone(f0,f1,dur,type,vol,delay){if(!AC||muted)return;try{const t=AC.currentTime+(delay||0);const o=AC.createOscillator(),g=AC.createGain();o.type=type;o.frequency.setValueAtTime(Math.max(1,f0),t);if(f1&&f1!==f0)o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(1,f1),t+dur);g.gain.setValueAtTime(vol,t);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0001,t+dur);o.connect(g);g.connect(master);o.start(t);o.stop(t+dur+.03);}catch(e){}}
const sfx={
 dot(){tone(dotFlip?760:640,dotFlip?980:820,.055,'square',.4);dotFlip=!dotFlip;},
 power(){tone(220,880,.35,'sawtooth',.5);tone(440,1760,.32,'square',.22,.03);},
 push(){tone(130,72,.09,'triangle',.7);},
 block(){tone(75,55,.05,'triangle',.3);},
 place(){tone(880,880,.08,'sine',.6);tone(1318,1318,.14,'sine',.5,.07);},
 unplace(){tone(320,210,.09,'triangle',.35);},
 eatGhost(){tone(980,140,.4,'sawtooth',.6);tone(1500,220,.3,'square',.2,.04);},
 death(){tone(700,55,.95,'sawtooth',.65);tone(520,48,.95,'square',.25,.08);},
 clear(){[523,659,784,1046].forEach((f,i)=>tone(f,f,.12,'square',.45,i*.09));tone(1318,1318,.3,'square',.35,.4);},
 extra(){[660,880,1100,1320].forEach((f,i)=>tone(f,f,.09,'square',.45,i*.07));},
 ui(){tone(500,760,.05,'square',.25);}
};
```

Level load:
```js
function loadLevel(i){
 levelIdx=i;const L=LEVELS[i];
 rowsN=L.rows.length;cols=Math.max.apply(null,L.rows.map(r=>r.length));
 walls=new Set();targets=new Set();pellets=new Map();boxes=[];
 for(let y=0;y<rowsN;y++){const row=L.rows[y]||'';
  for(let x=0;x<cols;x++){const c=x<row.length?row[x]:'#';const k=y*cols+x;
   if(c==='#')walls.add(k);
   else if(c==='o')pellets.set(k,'dot');
   else if(c==='O')pellets.set(k,'power');
   else if(c==='%')targets.add(k);
   else if(c==='$')boxes.push({x,y,px:x,py:y,t:1,on:false,pop:0});
   else if(c==='@'){pStart={x,y};}
  }}
 boxSet=new Set(boxes.map(b=>b.y*cols+b.x));
 boxes.forEach(b=>b.on=targets.has(b.y*cols+b.x));
 ghosts=(L.ghosts||[]).map((g,j)=>({x:g.x,y:g.y,px:g.x,py:g.y,sx:g.x,sy:g.y,t:1,lx:0,ly:-1,color:g.color,speed:g.speed,kind:g.kind,eaten:false,eatT:0,dizzyUntil:0,id:j}));
 player={x:pStart.x,y:pStart.y,px:pStart.x,py:pStart.y,t:1,face:{x:1,y:0},invulnUntil:T+900};
 frightT=0;chain=0;levelTime=0;dotsEaten=0;dotsTotal=pellets.size;particles=[];floaters=[];shake=0;buffer=null;held.length=0;
 updateHUD();resize();
}
```

```js
function restartLevel(){loadLevel(levelIdx);state='ready';stateT=0;showScreen(null);sfx.ui();}

function gameOver(){saveBest();state='over';$('#overStats').innerHTML=`SCORE <b>${score}</b><br>BEST <b>${best}</b> · REACHED LEVEL ${levelIdx+1}`;showScreen('over');}
```

```js
function addScore(n,big){score+=n;if(score>best)best=score; // live best display (not saved until end) — hmm saving only at end is fine, but live update of best display OK.
 if(!extraGiven&&score>=5000){extraGiven=true;lives++;sfx.extra();floaters.push({x:cols/2,y:rowsN/2,text:'EXTRA LIFE!',color:'#3dff9e',t:0,big:true});}
 updateHUD();}
```

Movement:
```js
function pickDir(){if(buffer&&T-buffer.t<400){const d=buffer.d;buffer=null;return d;}return held.length?held[held.length-1]:null;}
function tryMove(d){player.face=d;
 const nx=player.x+d.x,ny=player.y+d.y;if(nx<0||ny<0||nx>=cols||ny>=rowsN)return;
 if(walls.has(ny*cols+nx)){sfx.block();return;}
 const b=boxes.find(o=>o.x===nx&&o.y===ny);
 if(b){const bx=nx+d.x,by=ny+d.y;
  if(bx<0||by<0||bx>=cols||by>=rowsN||walls.has(by*cols+bx)||boxes.some(o=>o!==b&&o.x===bx&&o.y===by)){sfx.block();return;}
  b.px=b.x;b.py=b.y;b.t=0;
 }
 player.px=player.x;player.py=player.y;player.x=nx;player.y=ny;player.t=0;
 if(b)sfx.push();}
function playerArrive(){const k=player.y*cols+player.x;if(!pellets.has(k))return;
 const p=pellets.get(k);pellets.delete(k);dotsEaten++;runDots++;
 const cx=(player.x+.5)*tile, cy=(player.y+.5)*tile;
 if(p==='dot'){addScore(10);sfx.dot();spark(cx,cy,'#ffe9a8',4);}
 else{addScore(50);chain=1;frightT=LEVELS[levelIdx].fright*1000;sfx.power();ringFx(cx,cy,'#ffd93b');floaters.push({x:player.x+.5,y:player.y,text:'FEAST!',color:'#ffd93b',t:0});}
 if(pellets.size===0){addScore(150);floaters.push({x:player.x+.5,y:player.y-.6,text:'+150 ALL DOTS',color:'#7df9ff',t:0});}
 updateHUD();}
function boxArrive(b){const on=targets.has(b.y*cols+b.x);
 if(on&&!b.on){b.pop=1;addScore(100);sfx.place();ringFx((b.x+.5)*tile,(b.y+.5)*tile,'#3dff9e');spark((b.x+.5)*tile,(b.y+.5)*tile,'#3dff9e',10);floaters.push({x:b.x+.5,y:b.y-.2,text:'+100',color:'#3dff9e',t:0});shake=Math.max(shake,.3);}
 else if(!on&&b.on){sfx.unplace();}
 b.on=on;updateHUD();
 let all=true;for(const k of targets){if(!boxes.some(o=>o.x===k%cols&&o.y===(k-k%cols)/cols)){all=false;break;}}
 if(all)beginClear();}
function beginClear(){const secs=Math.floor(levelTime/1000);const bonus=Math.max(0,LEVELS[levelIdx].par-secs)*10;if(bonus>0)addScore(bonus);sfx.clear();state='clear';stateT=0;
 $('#clearStats').innerHTML=`TIME <b>${secs}s</b> · BONUS <b>+${bonus}</b><br>DOTS <b>${dotsEaten}/${dotsTotal}</b>`;showScreen('clear');}
```

Ghosts:
```js
function bfsField(sx,sy){const dist=new Int16Array(cols*rowsN).fill(-1);if(walls.has(sy*cols+sx))return null;const q=[sy*cols+sx];dist[sy*cols+sx]=0;let head=0;
 while(head<q.length){const ci=q[head++],cx=ci%cols,cy=(ci-cx)/cols,cd=dist[ci];
  for(let i=0;i<4;i++){const d=DIRS[i],nx=cx+d.x,ny=cy+d.y;if(nx<0||ny<0||nx>=cols||ny>=rowsN)continue;const ni=ny*cols+nx;if(dist[ni]!==-1||walls.has(ni)||boxSet.has(ni))continue;dist[ni]=cd+1;q.push(ni);}}
 return dist;}
function brainTarget(g,menu){if(menu)return null;
 if(g.kind==='chase')return{x:player.x,y:player.y};
 if(g.kind==='ambush'){let tx=player.x+player.face.x*2,ty=player.y+player.face.y*2;if(tx<0||ty<0||tx>=cols||ty>=rowsN||walls.has(ty*cols+tx)){tx=player.x+player.face.x;ty=player.y+player.face.y;}return{x:tx,y:ty};}
 return Math.random()<.5?{x:player.x,y:player.y}:null;}
function ghostStep(g,menu){const opts=[];
 for(let i=0;i<4;i++){const d=DIRS[i],nx=g.x+d.x,ny=g.y+d.y;if(nx<0||ny<0||nx>=cols||ny>=rowsN)continue;if(walls.has(ny*cols+nx)||boxSet.has(ny*cols+nx))continue;opts.push(d);}
 if(!opts.length)return;
 let pick=null;const rev=(d)=>d.x===-g.lx&&d.y===-g.ly;
 if(frightT>0&&!menu){
  if(Math.random()<.35)pick=opts[(Math.random()*opts.length)|0];
  else{const f=bfsField(player.x,player.y);let bd=-1,bt=[];
   for(const d of opts){const dd=f?((g.x+d.x)<0?-1:f[(g.y+d.y)*cols+(g.x+d.x)]):-1;if(dd<0)continue;const s=dd-(rev(d)?.35:0);if(s>bd+1e-9){bd=s;bt=[d];}else if(Math.abs(s-bd)<1e-9)bt.push(d);}
   pick=bt.length?bt[(Math.random()*bt.length)|0]:opts[(Math.random()*opts.length)|0];}}
 else{const tgt=brainTarget(g,menu);
  if(tgt){const f=bfsField(tgt.x,tgt.y);let bd=Infinity,bt=[];
   for(const d of opts){const dd=f?f[(g.y+d.y)*cols+(g.x+d.x)]:-1;if(dd<0)continue;const s=dd+(rev(d)?.35:0);if(s<bd-1e-9){bd=s;bt=[d];}else if(Math.abs(s-bd)<1e-9)bt.push(d);}
   pick=bt.length?bt[(Math.random()*bt.length)|0]:null;}
  if(!pick){const nr=opts.filter(d=>!rev(d));const pool=nr.length?nr:opts;pick=pool[(Math.random()*pool.length)|0];}}
 g.px=g.x;g.py=g.y;g.x+=pick.x;g.y+=pick.y;g.lx=pick.x;g.ly=pick.y;g.t=0;}
function updateGhosts(dt,menu){boxSet=new Set();for(const b of boxes)boxSet.add(b.y*cols+b.x);
 for(const g of ghosts){
  if(g.eaten){if(!menu){g.eatT+=dt;if(g.eatT>=2600){g.eaten=false;g.x=g.sx;g.y=g.sy;g.px=g.x;g.py=g.y;g.t=1;g.dizzyUntil=T+2200;}}continue;}
  const int=(menu?g.speed*2:(frightT>0?g.speed*1.7:g.speed));
  if(!menu&&T<g.dizzyUntil){g.t=1;continue;}
  if(g.t<1)g.t=Math.min(1,g.t+dt/int);
  if(g.t>=1)ghostStep(g,menu);}}
```
Wait: bfsField returns Int16Array (indexed) — I changed return to raw array; in ghostStep I index directly ✓. Also for target tile itself being a box? Player is never on a box ✓; ambush target could be a wall → handled by fallback to 1 ahead or... if 1-ahead also wall, tgt = wall tile → bfsField returns null (start is wall) → f=null → all dd=-1 → bt empty → pick null → wander ✓.

Collision:
```js
function lerpP(e){return{x:e.px+(e.x-e.px)*e.t,y:e.py+(e.y-e.py)*e.t};}
function checkCollisions(){if(state!=='play')return;const p=lerpP(player);
 for(const g of ghosts){if(g.eaten||T<g.dizzyUntil)continue;const gp=lerpP(g);const dx=gp.x-p.x,dy=gp.y-p.y;
  if(dx*dx+dy*dy<.38){ // .62^2≈.384
   if(frightT>0)eatGhost(g);
   else if(T>=player.invulnUntil)die();
   else eatGhost? no — invuln means nothing (ghost passes through). 
  }}}
```
If invuln and not frightened → ignore (no death, no eat). ✓

eatGhost / die:
```js
function eatGhost(g){g.eaten=true;g.eatT=0;runGhosts++;const pts=200*chain;addScore(pts);sfx.eatGhost();shake=Math.max(shake,.5);poof((g.x+.5)*tile,(g.y+.5)*tile,g.color);floaters.push({x:g.x+.5,y:g.y-.3,text:'+'+pts+(chain>1?' ×'+chain:''),color:'#7df9ff',t:0});}
function die(){state='dying';stateT=0;sfx.death();shake=1.2;shards((player.x+.5)*tile,(player.y+.5)*tile);showScreen(null);}
function afterDeath(){lives--;updateHUD();if(lives<=0){gameOver();return;}
 player.x=pStart.x;player.y=pStart.y;player.px=pStart.x;player.py=pStart.y;player.t=1;player.invulnUntil=T+2000;
 ghosts.forEach(g=>{g.x=g.sx;g.y=g.sy;g.px=g.sx;g.py=g.sy;g.t=1;g.eaten=false;g.dizzyUntil=T+3000;});
 frightT=0;chain=0;state='ready';stateT=0;}
```

Particles:
```js
function spark(x,y,c,n){for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const a=Math.random()*6.283,s=40+Math.random()*130;particles.push({type:'s',x,y,vx:Math.cos(a)*s,vy:Math.sin(a)*s,life:.5,max:.5,c,r:1.5+Math.random()*2});}}
function ringFx(x,y,c){particles.push({type:'r',x,y,t:0,max:.5,c});}
function poof(x,y,c){for(let i=0;i<12;i++){const a=Math.random()*6.283,s=20+Math.random()*60;particles.push({type:'p',x,y,vx:Math.cos(a)*s,vy:Math.sin(a)*s-30,life:.7,max:.7,c,r:3+Math.random()*4});}}
function shards(x,y){for(let i=0;i<18;i++){const a=Math.random()*6.283,s=80+Math.random()*220;particles.push({type:'s',x,y,vx:Math.cos(a)*s,vy:Math.sin(a)*s,life:.9,max:.9,c:i%2?'#ffd93b':'#ff5a6e',r:2+Math.random()*3});}}
function updateParticles(dt){const s=dt/1000;
 for(let i=particles.length-1;i>=0;i--){const p=particles[i];p.life-=s;if(p.life<=0){particles.splice(i,1);continue;}
  if(p.type==='r'){p.t+=s;continue;}
  p.x+=p.vx*s;p.y+=p.vy*s;p.vx*=Math.pow(.02,s)? use drag: p.vx*=(1-3*s) clamp; simpler p.vx*= (1-Math.min(1,4*s)); p.vy=p.vy*(1-Math.min(1,4*s)) + (p.type==='s'? 60*s:0); }
 for(let i=floaters.length-1;i>=0;i--){floaters[i].t+=dt;if(floaters[i].t>950)floaters.splice(i,1);}
 shake=Math.max(0,shake-dt/700? use exponential: shake*=Math.pow(.03,dt/1000)? let me do shake-=shake*6*dt/1000); boxes.forEach(b=>{if(b.pop>0)b.pop=Math.max(0,b.pop-dt/380);});}
```

Update main:
```js
function update(dt){
 if(state==='menu'){updateGhosts(dt,true);}
 else if(state==='ready'){stateT+=dt;if(stateT>=READY_MS)state='play';}
 else if(state==='play'){levelTime+=dt;
  if(frightT>0){frightT-=dt;if(frightT<=0){frightT=0;chain=0;}}
  const was=player.t;player.t=Math.min(1,player.t+dt/STEP);
  if(was<1&&player.t>=1)playerArrive();
  for(const b of boxes){if(b.t<1){const bw=b.t;b.t=Math.min(1,b.t+dt/STEP);if(bw<1&&b.t>=1)boxArrive(b);}}
  if(player.t>=1){const d=pickDir();if(d)tryMove(d);}
  updateGhosts(dt,false);
  checkCollisions();}
 else if(state==='dying'){stateT+=dt;if(stateT>=1100)afterDeath();}
 else if(state==='clear'){stateT+=dt;if(stateT>=2600)nextLevel();}
 updateParticles(dt);}
```
Order note: tryMove sets player.t=0; the arrival→move in same frame is fine.

Hmm — potential issue: player.t stays 1 while idle (no input) and pickDir returns held dir each frame → continuous movement ✓ intended.

Render functions as planned. Let me write drawGhost fully:

```js
function drawGhost(g){if(g.eaten)return;const e=g.t<1?g.t:1;const cx=ox+(g.px+(g.x-g.px)*e)*tile+tile/2;let cy=oy+(g.py+(g.y-g.py)*e)*tile+tile/2;cy+=Math.sin(T/160+g.id*2.1)*tile*.045;
 let alpha=1;if(!state==='menu'... dizzy blink: if(T<g.dizzyUntil&&(T/150|0)%2===0)alpha=.35;
 const r=tile*.42;const fright=frightT>0&&state!=='menu';
 let body=fright?('#3d55ff'):(g.color);
 if(fright&&frightT<1800&&(T/170|0)%2===0)body='#eef4ff';
 ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=alpha;
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(cx,cy-r*.12,r,Math.PI,Math.PI*2,false);const by=cy+r*.8;ctx.lineTo(cx+r,by);const w=2*r/3;for(let i=0;i<3;i++){const bx=cx+r-(i+.5)*w;ctx.arc(bx,by,w/2,0,Math.PI,true);}ctx.closePath();
 const grd=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,cy-r,0,cy+r);grd.addColorStop(0,body);grd.addColorStop(1, shade darker? just add stop with rgba black .15: use ctx.fillStyle=body and then overlay? simpler fill body then subtle top gloss: skip.
 ctx.fillStyle=body;ctx.shadowColor=body;ctx.shadowBlur=tile*.3;ctx.fill();ctx.shadowBlur=0;
 if(fright){ // frightened face
  ctx.fillStyle='#eaf2ff';const er=r*.16;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(cx-r*.35,cy-r*.2,er,0,7);ctx.arc(cx+r*.35,cy-r*.2,er,0,7);ctx.fill();
  ctx.strokeStyle='#eaf2ff';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1.5,r*.14);ctx.beginPath();const my=cy+r*.28;for(let i=0;i<=6;i++){const mx=cx-r*.55+i*(r*1.1/6);const myy=my+((i%2)?-r*.12:r*.12);if(i===0)ctx.moveTo(mx,myy);else ctx.lineTo(mx,myy);}ctx.stroke();
 } else { // eyes toward player
  const pp=lerpP(player);let dx=pp.x-(g.px+(g.x-g.px)*e),dy=pp.y-(g.py+(g.y-g.py)*e);const m=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1;dx=dx/m*r*.13;dy=dy/m*r*.13;
  ctx.fillStyle='#fff';ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(cx-r*.4,cy-r*.25,r*.3,r*.36,0,0,7);ctx.ellipse(cx+r*.4,cy-r*.25,r*.3,r*.36,0,0,7);ctx.fill();
  ctx.fillStyle='#1b2a8a';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(cx-r*.4+dx,cy-r*.25+dy,r*.15,0,7);ctx.arc(cx+r*.4+dx,cy-r*.25+dy,r*.15,0,7);ctx.fill();
 }
 ctx.restore();}
```
Note: two ellipse calls in one path with fill — the second ellipse adds a subpath; fill fills both ✓ (nonzero). But arcs without moveTo between create connecting lines — for circles it's fine visually? With fill nonzero and overlapping... they don't overlap; the connecting line from end of first ellipse to start of second might draw as filled sliver? Path: ellipse() starts at angle 0 point and goes around, ends where started. Then calling ellipse again continues path with a line from current point (end of first ellipse) to start of second → creates a thin line segment that gets filled as part of the closed subpath — could create a visible line between eyes! Safer: separate beginPath for each eye. Let me do:
```js
ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(e1...);ctx.fill();ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(e2...);ctx.fill();
```
Same for pupils (two arcs) → split, or use a single path with moveTo before the second arc. I'll just split fills — cheap.

Similarly frightened eyes: two circles in one path — same issue → split.

resize:
```js
function resize(){const r=stageEl.getBoundingClientRect();stageW=Math.max(50,r.width);stageH=Math.max(50,r.height);dpr=Math.min(2,window.devicePixelRatio||1);
 tile=Math.max(10,Math.floor(Math.min((stageW-28)/cols,(stageH-28)/rowsN)));
 ox=Math.round((stageW-tile*cols)/2);oy=Math.round((stageH-tile*rowsN)/2);
 cv.width=Math.round(stageW*dpr);cv.height=Math.round(stageH*dpr);cv.style.width=stageW+'px';cv.style.height=stageH+'px';
 buildStatic();}
window.addEventListener('resize',resize);
try{new ResizeObserver(resize).observe(stageEl);}catch(e){}
```
Note: canvas CSS border 2px adds to size? Canvas element with style width = stageW → total includes border → overflow by 4px; use box-sizing border-box on canvas? canvas replaced element + box-sizing:border-box makes the content area smaller... The *{box-sizing:border-box} applies; then style.width=stageW includes border ✓ good, internal bitmap is stageW*dpr but CSS content = stageW-4 → slight scale mismatch (0.7%) — negligible? drawImage uses transform dpr with coordinates in "CSS px" of canvas backing store... Actually ctx coordinates map to backing pixels via setTransform(dpr): 1 unit = 1 device px; visible CSS width = (stageW*dpr)/(dpr) minus border → content area = stageW-4 css px → our drawing coordinate space is stageW units wide but displayed in stageW-4 → everything scaled by ~0.993 — imperceptible. Fine.

Input:
```js
const KEYMAP={ArrowUp:[0,-1],KeyW:[0,-1],ArrowDown:[0,1],KeyS:[0,1],ArrowLeft:[-1,0],KeyA:[-1,0],ArrowRight:[1,0],KeyD:[1,0]};
function dirInHeld(d){return held.some(h=>h.x===d.x&&h.y===d.y);}
window.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{ensureAudio();const c=e.code;
 if(KEYMAP[c]){e.preventDefault();const d={x:KEYMAP[c][0],y:KEYMAP[c][1]};
  if(state==='menu'){startRun();}
  else if(state==='pause'){} // ignore movement while paused? or resume? keep ignore.
  buffer={d,t:T};if(!dirInHeld(d))held.push(d);return;}
 if(c==='Enter'||c==='Space'){e.preventDefault();primaryAction();return;}
 if(c==='KeyP'||c==='Escape'){togglePause();return;}
 if(c==='KeyR'){if(['play','ready','pause','dying'].includes(state))restartLevel();return;}
 if(c==='KeyM'){toggleMute();}});
window.addEventListener('keyup',e=>{const c=e.code;if(!KEYMAP[c])return;const d={x:KEYMAP[c][0],y:KEYMAP[c][1]};const i=held.findIndex(h=>h.x===d.x&&h.y===d.y);if(i>=0)held.splice(i,1);});
```

```js
function render(){ctx.setTransform(dpr,0,0,dpr,0,0);ctx.clearRect(0,0,stageW,stageH);ctx.fillStyle='#05081c';ctx.fillRect(0,0,stageW,stageH);
 if(staticCv)ctx.drawImage(staticCv,0,0,stageW,stageH);
 const shx=shake>.02?(Math.random()*2-1)*shake*6:0,shy=shake>.02?(Math.random()*2-1)*shake*6:0;
 ctx.save();ctx.translate(shx,shy);
 drawTargets();drawPellets();drawBoxes();for(const g of ghosts)drawGhost(g);drawPlayer();drawParticles();drawFloaters();
 ctx.restore();
 if(vigCv)ctx.drawImage(vigCv,0,0,stageW,stageH);
 if(state==='ready')drawReady();}
```

drawTargets:
```js
function drawTargets(){for(const k of targets){const x=k%cols,y=(k-k%cols)/cols;const cx=ox+x*tile+tile/2,cy=oy+y*tile+tile/2;const cov=boxes.some(b=>b.x===x&&b.y===y);
 if(cov){ctx.save();ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(61,255,158,.9)';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(2,tile*.06);ctx.shadowColor='#3dff9e';ctx.shadowBlur=tile*.3;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(cx,cy,tile*.36,0,7);ctx.stroke();ctx.restore();}
 else{const p=.5+.5*Math.sin(T/320+k*1.7);ctx.save();ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(61,255,158,'+(.3+.45*p)+')';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1.5,tile*.045);ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(cx,cy,tile*(.2+.06*p),0,7);ctx.stroke();ctx.fillStyle='rgba(61,255,158,'+(.25+.3*p)+')';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(cx,cy,Math.max(1.5,tile*.06),0,7);ctx.fill();ctx.restore();}}}
```

drawPellets:
```js
function drawPellets(){for(const[k,p]of pellets){const x=k%cols,y=(k-k%cols)/cols;const cx=ox+x*tile+tile/2,cy=oy+y*tile+tile/2;
 if(p==='dot'){const r=tile*.13;ctx.drawImage(dotSpr,cx-r,cy-r,r*2,r*2);}
 else{const pu=.5+.5*Math.sin(T/170+k);const r=tile*(.22+.09*pu);ctx.drawImage(powSpr,cx-r,cy-r,r*2,r*2);}}}
```

drawBoxes:
```js
function drawBoxes(){for(const b of boxes){const e=b.t<1?b.t*b.t*(3-2*b.t):1;const cx=ox+(b.px+(b.x-b.px)*e)*tile+tile/2,cy=oy+(b.py+(b.y-b.py)*e)*tile+tile/2;
 let sc=1;if(b.pop>0)sc=1+.16*Math.sin((1-b.pop)*Math.PI);const s=tile*.84*sc;
 const g=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,cy-s/2,0,cy+s/2);g.addColorStop(0,'#d79c58');g.addColorStop(1,'#96602e');
 rr(ctx,cx-s/2,cy-s/2,s,s,s*.16);ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.fill();
 ctx.strokeStyle='#5c3a17';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(2,s*.08);rr(ctx,cx-s/2,cy-s/2,s,s,s*.16);ctx.stroke();
 ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(60,34,10,.5)';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1.5,s*.05);const ip=s*.6;rr(ctx,cx-ip/2,cy-ip/2,ip,ip,s*.08);ctx.stroke();
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(cx-ip/2,cy-ip/2);ctx.lineTo(cx+ip/2,cy+ip/2);ctx.moveTo(cx+ip/2,cy-ip/2);ctx.lineTo(cx-ip/2,cy+ip/2);ctx.stroke();
 ctx.fillStyle='#f0c88e';const bo=s*.31,br=Math.max(1.5,s*.045);for(const[sx2,sy2]of[[-bo,-bo],[bo,-bo],[-bo,bo],[bo,bo]]){ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(cx+sx2,cy+sy2,br,0,7);ctx.fill();}
 if(b.on){ctx.save();ctx.shadowColor='#3dff9e';ctx.shadowBlur=s*.45;ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(61,255,158,.85)';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(2,s*.06);rr(ctx,cx-s/2,cy-s/2,s,s,s*.16);ctx.stroke();ctx.restore();}}}
```

```html
<script>
(function(){
'use strict';
/* ================= helpers ================= */
var $=function(s){return document.querySelector(s);};
var cv=$('#cv'),ctx=cv.getContext('2d'),stageEl=$('#stage');
var DIRS=[{x:1,y:0},{x:-1,y:0},{x:0,y:1},{x:0,y:-1}];
function rr(c,x,y,w,h,r){r=Math.min(r,w/2,h/2);c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x+r,y);c.arcTo(x+w,y,x+w,y+h,r);c.arcTo(x+w,y+h,x,y+h,r);c.arcTo(x,y+h,x,y,r);c.arcTo(x,y,x+w,y,r);c.closePath();}

/* ============ LEVELS (hand-verified solvable) ============ */
```
