```html
/* ================= state ================= */
var T=0,lastTs=0;
var levelIdx=0,cols=0,rowsN=0;
var walls,targets,pellets,boxes,boxSet,pStart;
var player=null,ghosts=[];
var score=0,best=0,lives=3,chain=0,frightT=0,extraGiven=false,newBestFlag=false;
var state='boot',stateT=0,pausedFrom='play',levelTime=0,dotsEaten=0,dotsTotal=0,runDots=0,runGhosts=0;
var particles=[],floaters=[],shake=0;
var tile=32,ox=0,oy=0,stageW=0,stageH=0,dpr=1;
var staticCv=null,vigCv=null,dotSpr,powSpr;
var STEP=135,READY_MS=1400;
var buffer=null,held=[];
try{best=parseInt(localStorage.getItem('pacoban.best')||'0',10)||0;}catch(e){}

/* ================= sprites ================= */
function makeSpr(fn){var s=document.createElement('canvas');s.width=s.height=48;fn(s.getContext('2d'));return s;}
dotSpr=makeSpr(function(g){var gr=g.createRadialGradient(24,24,1,24,24,23);gr.addColorStop(0,'#fffbe9');gr.addColorStop(.4,'#ffe9a8');gr.addColorStop(.65,'rgba(255,214,110,.35)');gr.addColorStop(1,'rgba(255,214,110,0)');g.fillStyle=gr;g.beginPath();g.arc(24,24,23,0,7);g.fill();});
powSpr=makeSpr(function(g){var gr=g.createRadialGradient(24,24,1,24,24,23);gr.addColorStop(0,'#fff');gr.addColorStop(.35,'#ffd93b');gr.addColorStop(.7,'rgba(255,176,46,.3)');gr.addColorStop(1,'rgba(255,176,46,0)');g.fillStyle=gr;g.beginPath();g.arc(24,24,23,0,7);g.fill();g.strokeStyle='rgba(255,230,150,.9)';g.lineWidth=3;g.beginPath();g.arc(24,24,15,0,7);g.stroke();});

/* ================= audio ================= */
var AC=null,master=null,muted=false,dotFlip=false;
function ensureAudio(){if(AC)return;try{var Ctx=window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext;if(!Ctx)return;AC=new Ctx();master=AC.createGain();master.gain.value=.16;master.connect(AC.destination);}catch(e){}}
function tone(f0,f1,dur,type,vol,delay){if(!AC||muted)return;try{var t=AC.currentTime+(delay||0);var o=AC.createOscillator(),g=AC.createGain();o.type=type;o.frequency.setValueAtTime(Math.max(1,f0),t);if(f1&&f1!==f0)o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(1,f1),t+dur);g.gain.setValueAtTime(vol,t);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0001,t+dur);o.connect(g);g.connect(master);o.start(t);o.stop(t+dur+.03);}catch(e){}}
var sfx={
 dot:function(){tone(dotFlip?760:640,dotFlip?980:820,.055,'square',.4);dotFlip=!dotFlip;},
 power:function(){tone(220,880,.35,'sawtooth',.5);tone(440,1760,.32,'square',.22,.03);},
 push:function(){tone(130,72,.09,'triangle',.7);},
 block:function(){tone(75,55,.05,'triangle',.3);},
 place:function(){tone(880,880,.08,'sine',.6);tone(1318,1318,.14,'sine',.5,.07);},
 unplace:function(){tone(320,210,.09,'triangle',.35);},
 eatGhost:function(){tone(980,140,.4,'sawtooth',.6);tone(1500,220,.3,'square',.2,.04);},
 death:function(){tone(700,55,.95,'sawtooth',.65);tone(520,48,.95,'square',.25,.08);},
 clear:function(){[523,659,784,1046].forEach(function(f,i){tone(f,f,.12,'square',.45,i*.09);});tone(1318,1318,.3,'square',.35,.4);},
 extra:function(){[660,880,1100,1320].forEach(function(f,i){tone(f,f,.09,'square',.45,i*.07);});},
 ui:function(){tone(500,760,.05,'square',.25);}
};

/* ================= HUD ================= */
var elScore=$('#hudScore'),elBest=$('#hudBest'),elCrates=$('#hudCrates'),elDotsF=$('#hudDotsFill'),elLvl=$('#hudLevel'),elLives=$('#hudLives');
var PAC_SVG='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M12 12L21.5 6.5A11 11 0 1 0 21.5 17.5Z" fill="#ffd93b"/></svg>';
function onCount(){var n=0;for(var i=0;i<boxes.length;i++)if(boxes[i].on)n++;return n;}
function updateHUD(){
 elScore.textContent=score;elBest.textContent=best;
 elCrates.textContent=onCount()+'/'+targets.size;
 elDotsF.style.width=(dotsTotal?Math.round(dotsEaten/dotsTotal*100):0)+'%';
 elLvl.textContent='LEVEL '+(levelIdx+1)+' · '+LEVELS[levelIdx].name;
 var h='',n=Math.min(lives,6);for(var i=0;i<n;i++)h+=PAC_SVG;if(lives>6)h+='<b style="color:#ffd93b">×'+lives+'</b>';
 elLives.innerHTML=h;$('#bestStart').textContent=best;
}

/* ================= screens ================= */
var scrEls={start:$('#scr-start'),pause:$('#scr-pause'),over:$('#scr-over'),clear:$('#scr-clear'),win:$('#scr-win')};
function showScreen(n){for(var k in scrEls)scrEls[k].classList.toggle('hidden',k!==n);}

/* ================= level load ================= */
function loadLevel(i){
 levelIdx=i;var L=LEVELS[i];
 rowsN=L.rows.length;cols=Math.max.apply(null,L.rows.map(function(r){return r.length;}));
 walls=new Set();targets=new Set();pellets=new Map();boxes=[];pStart={x:1,y:1};
 for(var y=0;y<rowsN;y++){var row=L.rows[y]||'';
  for(var x=0;x<cols;x++){var c=x<row.length?row[x]:'#';var k=y*cols+x;
   if(c==='#')walls.add(k);
   else if(c==='o')pellets.set(k,'dot');
   else if(c==='O')pellets.set(k,'power');
   else if(c==='%')targets.add(k);
   else if(c==='$')boxes.push({x:x,y:y,px:x,py:y,t:1,on:false,pop:0});
   else if(c==='@')pStart={x:x,y:y};
  }}
 boxSet=new Set(boxes.map(function(b){return b.y*cols+b.x;}));
 boxes.forEach(function(b){b.on=targets.has(b.y*cols+b.x);});
 ghosts=(L.ghosts||[]).map(function(g,j){return {x:g.x,y:g.y,px:g.x,py:g.y,sx:g.x,sy:g.y,t:1,lx:0,ly:-1,color:g.color,speed:g.speed,kind:g.kind,eaten:false,eatT:0,dizzyUntil:0,id:j};});
 player={x:pStart.x,y:pStart.y,px:pStart.x,py:pStart.y,t:1,face:{x:1,y:0},invulnUntil:T+900};
 frightT=0;chain=0;levelTime=0;dotsEaten=0;dotsTotal=pellets.size;
 particles=[];floaters=[];shake=0;buffer=null;held.length=0;
 updateHUD();resize();
}

/* ================= run control ================= */
function startRun(){score=0;lives=3;extraGiven=false;runDots=0;runGhosts=0;newBestFlag=false;loadLevel(0);state='ready';stateT=0;showScreen(null);sfx.ui();}
function restartLevel(){loadLevel(levelIdx);state='ready';stateT=0;showScreen(null);sfx.ui();}
function nextLevel(){if(levelIdx+1<LEVELS.length){loadLevel(levelIdx+1);state='ready';stateT=0;}else winGame();}
function saveBest(){if(score>best){best=score;newBestFlag=true;try{localStorage.setItem('pacoban.best',String(best));}catch(e){}}updateHUD();}
function gameOver(){saveBest();state='over';$('#overStats').innerHTML='SCORE <b>'+score+'</b><br>BEST <b>'+best+'</b> · REACHED LEVEL '+(levelIdx+1);showScreen('over');}
function winGame(){saveBest();state='win';$('#newBest').classList.toggle('hidden',!newBestFlag);
 $('#winStats').innerHTML='FINAL SCORE <b>'+score+'</b><br>BEST <b>'+best+'</b> · DOTS <b>'+runDots+'</b> · GHOSTS EATEN <b>'+runGhosts+'</b>';
 showScreen('win');sfx.clear();spark(stageW/2,stageH/3,'#ffd93b',26);spark(stageW/2,stageH/3,'#7df9ff',20);}
function addScore(n){score+=n;if(score>best)best=score;
 if(!extraGiven&&score>=5000){extraGiven=true;lives++;sfx.extra();floaters.push({x:cols/2,y:rowsN/2,text:'EXTRA LIFE!',color:'#3dff9e',t:0,big:true});}
 updateHUD();}

/* ================= movement / sokoban core ================= */
function pickDir(){if(buffer&&T-buffer.t<400){var d=buffer.d;buffer=null;return d;}return held.length?held[held.length-1]:null;}
function tryMove(d){player.face=d;
 var nx=player.x+d.x,ny=player.y+d.y;
 if(nx<0||ny<0||nx>=cols||ny>=rowsN)return;
 if(walls.has(ny*cols+nx)){sfx.block();return;}
 var b=null;for(var i=0;i<boxes.length;i++){if(boxes[i].x===nx&&boxes[i].y===ny){b=boxes[i];break;}}
 if(b){var bx=nx+d.x,by=ny+d.y;
  if(bx<0||by<0||bx>=cols||by>=rowsN||walls.has(by*cols+bx)||boxes.some(function(o){return o!==b&&o.x===bx&&o.y===by;})){sfx.block();return;}
  b.px=b.x;b.py=b.y;b.t=0;}
 player.px=player.x;player.py=player.y;player.x=nx;player.y=ny;player.t=0;
 if(b)sfx.push();}
function playerArrive(){var k=player.y*cols+player.x;if(!pellets.has(k))return;
 var p=pellets.get(k);pellets.delete(k);dotsEaten++;runDots++;
 var cx=(player.x+.5)*tile,cy=(player.y+.5)*tile;
 if(p==='dot'){addScore(10);sfx.dot();spark(cx,cy,'#ffe9a8',4);}
 else{addScore(50);chain=1;frightT=LEVELS[levelIdx].fright*1000;sfx.power();ringFx(cx,cy,'#ffd93b');floaters.push({x:player.x+.5,y:player.y,text:'FEAST!',color:'#ffd93b',t:0});}
 if(pellets.size===0){addScore(150);floaters.push({x:player.x+.5,y:player.y-.6,text:'+150 ALL DOTS',color:'#7df9ff',t:0});}
 updateHUD();}
function boxArrive(b){var on=targets.has(b.y*cols+b.x);
 if(on&&!b.on){b.pop=1;addScore(100);sfx.place();ringFx((b.x+.5)*tile,(b.y+.5)*tile,'#3dff9e');spark((b.x+.5)*tile,(b.y+.5)*tile,'#3dff9e',10);floaters.push({x:b.x+.5,y:b.y-.2,text:'+100',color:'#3dff9e',t:0});shake=Math.max(shake,.3);}
 else if(!on&&b.on){sfx.unplace();}
 b.on=on;updateHUD();
 for(var k of targets){var tx=k%cols,ty=(k-tx)/cols;var ok=false;for(var i=0;i<boxes.length;i++){if(boxes[i].x===tx&&boxes[i].y===ty){ok=true;break;}}if(!ok)return;}
 beginClear();}
function beginClear(){var secs=Math.floor(levelTime/1000);var bonus=Math.max(0,LEVELS[levelIdx].par-secs)*10;if(bonus>0)addScore(bonus);
 sfx.clear();state='clear';stateT=0;
 $('#clearStats').innerHTML='TIME <b>'+secs+'s</b> · BONUS <b>+'+bonus+'</b><br>DOTS <b>'+dotsEaten+'/'+dotsTotal+'</b>';showScreen('clear');}

/* ================= ghosts (pac-man core) ================= */
function bfsField(sx,sy){var dist=new Int16Array(cols*rowsN).fill(-1);if(walls.has(sy*cols+sx))return null;
 var q=[sy*cols+sx];dist[sy*cols+sx]=0;var head=0;
 while(head<q.length){var ci=q[head++],cx=ci%cols,cy=(ci-cx)/cols,cd=dist[ci];
  for(var i=0;i<4;i++){var d=DIRS[i],nx=cx+d.x,ny=cy+d.y;if(nx<0||ny<0||nx>=cols||ny>=rowsN)continue;
   var ni=ny*cols+nx;if(dist[ni]!==-1||walls.has(ni)||boxSet.has(ni))continue;dist[ni]=cd+1;q.push(ni);}}
 return dist;}
function brainTarget(g,menu){if(menu)return null;
 if(g.kind==='chase')return {x:player.x,y:player.y};
 if(g.kind==='ambush'){var tx=player.x+player.face.x*2,ty=player.y+player.face.y*2;
  if(tx<0||ty<0||tx>=cols||ty>=rowsN||walls.has(ty*cols+tx)){tx=player.x+player.face.x;ty=player.y+player.face.y;}return {x:tx,y:ty};}
 return Math.random()<.5?{x:player.x,y:player.y}:null;}
function ghostStep(g,menu){var opts=[];
 for(var i=0;i<4;i++){var d=DIRS[i],nx=g.x+d.x,ny=g.y+d.y;if(nx<0||ny<0||nx>=cols||ny>=rowsN)continue;if(walls.has(ny*cols+nx)||boxSet.has(ny*cols+nx))continue;opts.push(d);}
 if(!opts.length)return;
 var pick=null,rev=function(d){return d.x===-g.lx&&d.y===-g.ly;};
 if(frightT>0&&!menu){
  if(Math.random()<.35)pick=opts[(Math.random()*opts.length)|0];
  else{var f=bfsField(player.x,player.y);var bd=-1,bt=[];
   for(var i=0;i<opts.length;i++){var d=opts[i],dd=f?f[(g.y+d.y)*cols+(g.x+d.x)]:-1;if(dd<0)continue;var s=dd-(rev(d)?.35:0);
    if(s>bd+1e-9){bd=s;bt=[d];}else if(Math.abs(s-bd)<1e-9)bt.push(d);}
   pick=bt.length?bt[(Math.random()*bt.length)|0]:opts[(Math.random()*opts.length)|0];}}
 else{var tgt=brainTarget(g,menu);
  if(tgt){var f2=bfsField(tgt.x,tgt.y);var bd2=Infinity,bt2=[];
   for(var j=0;j<opts.length;j++){var d2=opts[j],dd2=f2?f2[(g.y+d2.y)*cols+(g.x+d2.x)]:-1;if(dd2<0)continue;var s2=dd2+(rev(d2)?.35:0);
    if(s2<bd2-1e-9){bd2=s2;bt2=[d2];}else if(Math.abs(s2-bd2)<1e-9)bt2.push(d2);}
   pick=bt2.length?bt2[(Math.random()*bt2.length)|0]:null;}
  if(!pick){var nr=opts.filter(function(d){return !rev(d);});var pool=nr.length?nr:opts;pick=pool[(Math.random()*pool.length)|0];}}
 g.px=g.x;g.py=g.y;g.x+=pick.x;g.y+=pick.y;g.lx=pick.x;g.ly=pick.y;g.t=0;}
function updateGhosts(dt,menu){boxSet=new Set();for(var i=0;i<boxes.length;i++)boxSet.add(boxes[i].y*cols+boxes[i].x);
 for(var j=0;j<ghosts.length;j++){var g=ghosts[j];
  if(g.eaten){if(!menu){g.eatT+=dt;if(g.eatT>=2600){g.eaten=false;g.x=g.sx;g.y=g.sy;g.px=g.x;g.py=g.y;g.t=1;g.dizzyUntil=T+2200;}}continue;}
  var int=(menu?g.speed*2:(frightT>0?g.speed*1.7:g.speed));
  if(!menu&&T<g.dizzyUntil){g.t=1;continue;}
  if(g.t<1)g.t=Math.min(1,g.t+dt/int);
  if(g.t>=1)ghostStep(g,menu);}}
function lerpP(e){return {x:e.px+(e.x-e.px)*e.t,y:e.py+(e.y-e.py)*e.t};}
function checkCollisions(){if(state!=='play')return;var p=lerpP(player);
 for(var i=0;i<ghosts.length;i++){var g=ghosts[i];if(g.eaten||T<g.dizzyUntil)continue;
  var gp=lerpP(g),dx=gp.x-p.x,dy=gp.y-p.y;
  if(dx*dx+dy*dy<.38){
   if(frightT>0)eatGhost(g);
   else if(T>=player.invulnUntil)die();}}}
function eatGhost(g){g.eaten=true;g.eatT=0;runGhosts++;var pts=200*chain;addScore(pts);sfx.eatGhost();shake=Math.max(shake,.5);
 poof((g.x+.5)*tile,(g.y+.5)*tile,g.color);
 floaters.push({x:g.x+.5,y:g.y-.3,text:'+'+pts+(chain>1?' ×'+chain:''),color:'#7df9ff',t:0});}
function die(){state='dying';stateT=0;sfx.death();shake=1.2;shards((player.x+.5)*tile,(player.y+.5)*tile);showScreen(null);}
function afterDeath(){lives--;updateHUD();if(lives<=0){gameOver();return;}
 player.x=pStart.x;player.y=pStart.y;player.px=pStart.x;player.py=pStart.y;player.t=1;player.invulnUntil=T+2000;
 ghosts.forEach(function(g){g.x=g.sx;g.y=g.sy;g.px=g.sx;g.py=g.sy;g.t=1;g.eaten=false;g.dizzyUntil=T+3000;});
 frightT=0;chain=0;state='ready';stateT=0;}

/* ================= particles / floaters ================= */
function spark(x,y,c,n){for(var i=0;i<n;i++){var a=Math.random()*6.283,s=40+Math.random()*130;particles.push({type:'s',x:x,y:y,vx:Math.cos(a)*s,vy:Math.sin(a)*s,life:.5,max:.5,c:c,r:1.5+Math.random()*2});}}
function ringFx(x,y,c){particles.push({type:'r',x:x,y:y,t:0,max:.5,c:c});}
function poof(x,y,c){for(var i=0;i<12;i++){var a=Math.random()*6.283,s=20+Math.random()*60;particles.push({type:'p',x:x,y:y,vx:Math.cos(a)*s,vy:Math.sin(a)*s-30,life:.7,max:.7,c:c,r:3+Math.random()*4});}}
function shards(x,y){for(var i=0;i<18;i++){var a=Math.random()*6.283,s=80+Math.random()*220;particles.push({type:'s',x:x,y:y,vx:Math.cos(a)*s,vy:Math.sin(a)*s,life:.9,max:.9,c:i%2?'#ffd93b':'#ff5a6e',r:2+Math.random()*3});}}
function updateParticles(dt){var s=dt/1000;
 for(var i=particles.length-1;i>=0;i--){var p=particles[i];p.life-=s;if(p.life<=0){particles.splice(i,1);continue;}
  if(p.type==='r'){p.t+=s;continue;}
  var dr=Math.max(0,1-Math.min(1,4*s));p.x+=p.vx*s;p.y+=p.vy*s;p.vx*=dr;p.vy=p.vy*dr+60*s;}
 for(var j=floaters.length-1;j>=0;j--){floaters[j].t+=dt;if(floaters[j].t>950)floaters.splice(j,1);}
 shake-=shake*6*s;
 boxes.forEach(function(b){if(b.pop>0)b.pop=Math.max(0,b.pop-dt/380);});}

/* ================= update ================= */
function update(dt){
 if(state==='menu'){updateGhosts(dt,true);}
 else if(state==='ready'){stateT+=dt;if(stateT>=READY_MS)state='play';}
 else if(state==='play'){levelTime+=dt;
  if(frightT>0){frightT-=dt;if(frightT<=0){frightT=0;chain=0;}}
  var was=player.t;player.t=Math.min(1,player.t+dt/STEP);
  if(was<1&&player.t>=1)playerArrive();
  for(var i=0;i<boxes.length;i++){var b=boxes[i];if(b.t<1){var bw=b.t;b.t=Math.min(1,b.t+dt/STEP);if(bw<1&&b.t>=1)boxArrive(b);}}
  if(player.t>=1){var d=pickDir();if(d)tryMove(d);}
  updateGhosts(dt,false);checkCollisions();}
 else if(state==='dying'){stateT+=dt;if(stateT>=1100)afterDeath();}
 else if(state==='clear'){stateT+=dt;if(stateT>=2600)nextLevel();}
 updateParticles(dt);}

/* ================= static layer ================= */
function buildStatic(){if(stageW<10||stageH<10)return;
 staticCv=document.createElement('canvas');staticCv.width=cv.width;staticCv.height=cv.height;
 var c=staticCv.getContext('2d');c.setTransform(dpr,0,0,dpr,0,0);
 c.save();c.fillStyle='rgba(40,70,190,.13)';rr(c,ox-8,oy-8,tile*cols+16,tile*rowsN+16,tile*.5);c.fill();c.restore();
 for(var y=0;y<rowsN;y++)for(var x=0;x<cols;x++){var k=y*cols+x,px=ox+x*tile,py=oy+y*tile;
  if(walls.has(k)){var g=c.createLinearGradient(0,py,0,py+tile);g.addColorStop(0,'#2c3d86');g.addColorStop(1,'#141d4e');
   c.save();c.shadowColor='rgba(70,230,255,.3)';c.shadowBlur=tile*.22;rr(c,px+1.5,py+1.5,tile-3,tile-3,tile*.2);c.fillStyle=g;c.fill();c.restore();
   c.fillStyle='rgba(150,200,255,.2)';rr(c,px+tile*.2,py+tile*.14,tile*.6,tile*.1,tile*.05);c.fill();}
  else{var v=((x*7+y*11)%3);c.fillStyle=['#0a102e','#0b1233','#091028'][v];c.fillRect(px,py,tile,tile);
   c.fillStyle='rgba(110,150,255,.06)';c.beginPath();c.arc(px+tile/2,py+tile/2,Math.max(1,tile*.03),0,7);c.fill();}}
 vigCv=document.createElement('canvas');vigCv.width=cv.width;vigCv.height=cv.height;
 var v=vigCv.getContext('2d');v.setTransform(dpr,0,0,dpr,0,0);
 var vg=v.createRadialGradient(stageW/2,stageH/2,Math.min(stageW,stageH)*.35,stageW/2,stageH/2,Math.max(stageW,stageH)*.72);
 vg.addColorStop(0,'rgba(0,0,0,0)');vg.addColorStop(1,'rgba(2,4,16,.55)');v.fillStyle=vg;v.fillRect(0,0,stageW,stageH);}

function resize(){var r=stageEl.getBoundingClientRect();stageW=Math.max(50,r.width);stageH=Math.max(50,r.height);
 dpr=Math.min(2,window.devicePixelRatio||1);
 tile=Math.max(10,Math.floor(Math.min((stageW-28)/cols,(stageH-28)/rowsN)));
 ox=Math.round((stageW-tile*cols)/2);oy=Math.round((stageH-tile*rowsN)/2);
 cv.width=Math.round(stageW*dpr);cv.height=Math.round(stageH*dpr);cv.style.width=stageW+'px';cv.style.height=stageH+'px';
 buildStatic();}
window.addEventListener('resize',resize);
try{new ResizeObserver(resize).observe(stageEl);}catch(e){}

/* ================= render ================= */
function drawTargets(){for(var k of targets){var x=k%cols,y=(k-x)/cols;var cx=ox+x*tile+tile/2,cy=oy+y*tile+tile/2;
 var cov=false;for(var i=0;i<boxes.length;i++){if(boxes[i].x===x&&boxes[i].y===y){cov=true;break;}}
 if(cov){ctx.save();ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(61,255,158,.9)';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(2,tile*.06);ctx.shadowColor='#3dff9e';ctx.shadowBlur=tile*.3;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(cx,cy,tile*.36,0,7);ctx.stroke();ctx.restore();}
 else{var p=.5+.5*Math.sin(T/320+k*1.7);ctx.save();ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(61,255,158,'+(.3+.45*p)+')';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1.5,tile*.045);ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(cx,cy,tile*(.2+.06*p),0,7);ctx.stroke();
  ctx.fillStyle='rgba(61,255,158,'+(.25+.3*p)+')';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(cx,cy,Math.max(1.5,tile*.06),0,7);ctx.fill();ctx.restore();}}}
function drawPellets(){for(var e of pellets){var k=e[0],p=e[1];var x=k%cols,y=(k-x)/cols;var cx=ox+x*tile+tile/2,cy=oy+y*tile+tile/2;
 if(p==='dot'){var r=tile*.13;ctx.drawImage(dotSpr,cx-r,cy-r,r*2,r*2);}
 else{var pu=.5+.5*Math.sin(T/170+k);var r2=tile*(.22+.09*pu);ctx.drawImage(powSpr,cx-r2,cy-r2,r2*2,r2*2);}}}
function drawBoxes(){for(var i=0;i<boxes.length;i++){var b=boxes[i];var e=b.t<1?b.t*b.t*(3-2*b.t):1;
 var cx=ox+(b.px+(b.x-b.px)*e)*tile+tile/2,cy=oy+(b.py+(b.y-b.py)*e)*tile+tile/2;
 var sc=1;if(b.pop>0)sc=1+.16*Math.sin((1-b.pop)*Math.PI);var s=tile*.84*sc;
 var g=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,cy-s/2,0,cy+s/2);g.addColorStop(0,'#d79c58');g.addColorStop(1,'#96602e');
 rr(ctx,cx-s/2,cy-s/2,s,s,s*.16);ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.fill();
 ctx.strokeStyle='#5c3a17';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(2,s*.08);rr(ctx,cx-s/2,cy-s/2,s,s,s*.16);ctx.stroke();
 ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(60,34,10,.5)';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1.5,s*.05);var ip=s*.6;
 rr(ctx,cx-ip/2,cy-ip/2,ip,ip,s*.08);ctx.stroke();
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(cx-ip/2,cy-ip/2);ctx.lineTo(cx+ip/2,cy+ip/2);ctx.moveTo(cx+ip/2,cy-ip/2);ctx.lineTo(cx-ip/2,cy+ip/2);ctx.stroke();
 ctx.fillStyle='#f0c88e';var bo=s*.31,br=Math.max(1.5,s*.045);
 var pts=[[-bo,-bo],[bo,-bo],[-bo,bo],[bo,bo]];for(var j=0;j<4;j++){ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(cx+pts[j][0],cy+pts[j][1],br,0,7);ctx.fill();}
 if(b.on){ctx.save();ctx.shadowColor='#3dff9e';ctx.shadowBlur=s*.45;ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(61,255,158,.85)';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(2,s*.06);rr(ctx,cx-s/2,cy-s/2,s,s,s*.16);ctx.stroke();ctx.restore();}}}
function ghostPath(c,x,y,r){c.beginPath();c.arc(x,y-r*.12,r,Math.PI,Math.PI*2,false);var by=y+r*.8;c.lineTo(x+r,by);
 var w=2*r/3;for(var i=0;i<3;i++){var bx=x+r-(i+.5)*w;c.arc(bx,by,w/2,0,Math.PI,true);}c.closePath();}
function drawGhost(g){if(g.eaten)return;
 var e=g.t<1?g.t:1;
 var cx=ox+(g.px+(g.x-g.px)*e)*tile+tile/2;
 var cy=oy+(g.py+(g.y-g.py)*e)*tile+tile/2+Math.sin(T/160+g.id*2.1)*tile*.045;
 var alpha=1;if(T<g.dizzyUntil&&((T/150)|0)%2===0)alpha=.35;
 var fright=frightT>0&&state!=='menu';
 var body=fright?'#3d55ff':g.color;
 if(fright&&frightT<1800&&((T/170)|0)%2===0)body='#eef4ff';
 ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=alpha;
 ghostPath(ctx,cx,cy,tile*.42);
 ctx.fillStyle=body;ctx.shadowColor=body;ctx.shadowBlur=tile*.3;ctx.fill();ctx.shadowBlur=0;
 if(fright){
  ctx.fillStyle='#eaf2ff';var er=tile*.075;
  ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(cx-tile*.15,cy-tile*.09,er,0,7);ctx.fill();
  ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(cx+tile*.15,cy-tile*.09,er,0,7);ctx.fill();
  ctx.strokeStyle='#eaf2ff';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1.5,tile*.06);ctx.beginPath();var my=cy+tile*.13;
  for(var i=0;i<=6;i++){var mx=cx-tile*.24+i*(tile*.48/6),myy=my+((i%2)?-tile*.05:tile*.05);if(i===0)ctx.moveTo(mx,myy);else ctx.lineTo(mx,myy);}
  ctx.stroke();
 }else{
  var pp=lerpP(player);var dx=pp.x-(g.px+(g.x-g.px)*e),dy=pp.y-(g.py+(g.y-g.py)*e);
  var m=Math.sqrt(dx*dx+dy*dy)||1;dx=dx/m*tile*.055;dy=dy/m*tile*.055;
  ctx.fillStyle='#fff';
  ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(cx-tile*.17,cy-tile*.11,tile*.125,tile*.15,0,0,7);ctx.fill();
  ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(cx+tile*.17,cy-tile*.11,tile*.125,tile*.15,0,0,7);ctx.fill();
  ctx.fillStyle='#1b2a8a';
  ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(cx-tile*.17+dx,cy-tile*.11+dy,tile*.062,0,7);ctx.fill();
  ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(cx+tile*.17+dx,cy-tile*.11+dy,tile*.062,0,7);ctx.fill();}
 ctx.restore();}
function pacShape(x,y,r,ang){var g=ctx.createRadialGradient(x-r*.3,y-r*.3,r*.1,x,y,r);
 g.addColorStop(0,'#fff3a0');g.addColorStop(.55,'#ffd93b');g.addColorStop(1,'#f5b423');
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x,y);ctx.arc(x,y,r,ang,Math.PI*2-ang,false);ctx.closePath();ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.fill();}
function drawPlayer(){
 if(state==='dying'){var p=stateT/1100;if(p>=.55)return;
  var cx=ox+(player.x+.5)*tile,cy=oy+(player.y+.5)*tile;
  ctx.save();ctx.translate(cx,cy);ctx.rotate(p*14);var sc=Math.max(0,1-p*1.8);ctx.scale(sc,sc);ctx.globalAlpha=Math.max(0,1-p*1.7);
  pacShape(0,0,tile*.42,.5);ctx.restore();return;}
 var alpha=1;if(T<player.invulnUntil&&((T/120)|0)%2===0)alpha=.35;
 var e=player.t<1?player.t:1;
 var cx=ox+(player.px+(player.x-player.px)*e)*tile+tile/2;
 var cy=oy+(player.py+(player.y-player.py)*e)*tile+tile/2;
 var moving=player.t<1&&(state==='play');
 var ang=moving?.18+.5*Math.abs(Math.sin(T/85)):.14+.07*Math.sin(T/320);
 var rot=Math.atan2(player.face.y,player.face.x);
 ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=alpha;ctx.translate(cx,cy);ctx.rotate(rot);
 if(moving){ctx.shadowColor='rgba(255,217,59,.55)';ctx.shadowBlur=tile*.35;}
 pacShape(0,0,tile*.42,ang);ctx.restore();}
function drawParticles(){for(var i=0;i<particles.length;i++){var p=particles[i],a=Math.max(0,p.life/p.max);
 if(p.type==='r'){var pr=p.t/p.max,r2=tile*(.15+.6*pr);ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=a*.8;ctx.strokeStyle=p.c;ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(2,tile*.07*(1-pr));ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(p.x,p.y,r2,0,7);ctx.stroke();ctx.restore();continue;}
 ctx.save();ctx.globalCompositeOperation='lighter';ctx.globalAlpha=a;ctx.fillStyle=p.c;
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(p.x,p.y,p.r*(p.type==='p'?1.6:1),0,7);ctx.fill();ctx.restore();}}
function drawFloaters(){for(var i=0;i<floaters.length;i++){var f=floaters[i],a=Math.max(0,1-f.t/950);
 var px=ox+f.x*tile,py=oy+f.y*tile-(f.t/950)*26;
 ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=a;ctx.textAlign='center';
 ctx.font=(f.big?'900 ':'700 ')+Math.round(f.big?tile*.8:tile*.42)+"px 'Courier New',monospace";
 ctx.lineWidth=3;ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(5,8,24,.8)';ctx.strokeText(f.text,px,py);ctx.fillStyle=f.color;ctx.fillText(f.text,px,py);ctx.restore();}}
function drawReady(){var p=stateT/READY_MS;
 var a=p<.15?p/.15:(p>.85?Math.max(0,(1-p)/.15):1);
 var sc=.9+.25*Math.min(1,p*4);
 ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=a;ctx.translate(stageW/2,stageH/2);ctx.scale(sc,sc);
 ctx.font='900 '+Math.round(tile*1.3)+"px Impact,'Arial Black',sans-serif";ctx.textAlign='center';
 ctx.fillStyle='#ffd93b';ctx.shadowColor='rgba(70,230,255,.8)';ctx.shadowBlur=tile*.4;ctx.fillText('READY!',0,0);ctx.shadowBlur=0;
 ctx.font="700 "+Math.round(tile*.34)+"px 'Courier New',monospace";ctx.fillStyle='#9fd8ff';
 ctx.fillText('LEVEL '+(levelIdx+1)+' — '+LEVELS[levelIdx].name,0,tile*.72);ctx.restore();}
function render(){ctx.setTransform(dpr,0,0,dpr,0,0);
 ctx.clearRect(0,0,stageW,stageH);ctx.fillStyle='#05081c';ctx.fillRect(0,0,stageW,stageH);
 if(staticCv)ctx.drawImage(staticCv,0,0,stageW,stageH);
 var shx=shake>.02?(Math.random()*2-1)*shake*6:0,shy=shake>.02?(Math.random()*2-1)*shake*6:0;
 ctx.save();ctx.translate(shx,shy);
 drawTargets();drawPellets();drawBoxes();
 for(var i=0;i<ghosts.length;i++)drawGhost(ghosts[i]);
 drawPlayer();drawParticles();drawFloaters();
 ctx.restore();
 if(vigCv)ctx.drawImage(vigCv,0,0,stageW,stageH);
 if(state==='ready')drawReady();}

/* ================= input ================= */
var KEYMAP={ArrowUp:[0,-1],KeyW:[0,-1],ArrowDown:[0,1],KeyS:[0,1],ArrowLeft:[-1,0],KeyA:[-1,0],ArrowRight:[1,0],KeyD:[1,0]};
function dirInHeld(d){return held.some(function(h){return h.x===d.x&&h.y===d.y;});}
window.addEventListener('keydown',function(e){ensureAudio();var c=e.code;
 if(KEYMAP[c]){e.preventDefault();var d={x:KEYMAP[c][0],y:KEYMAP[c][1]};
  if(state==='menu')startRun();
  buffer={d:d,t:T};if(!dirInHeld(d))held.push(d);return;}
 if(c==='Enter'||c==='Space'){e.preventDefault();primaryAction();return;}
 if(c==='KeyP'||c==='Escape'){togglePause();return;}
 if(c==='KeyR'){if(['play','ready','pause','dying'].indexOf(state)>=0)restartLevel();return;}
 if(c==='KeyM')toggleMute();});
window.addEventListener('keyup',function(e){var c=e.code;if(!KEYMAP[c])return;
 var d={x:KEYMAP[c][0],y:KEYMAP[c][1]};var i=held.findIndex(function(h){return h.x===d.x&&h.y===d.y;});if(i>=0)held.splice(i,1);});
function primaryAction(){switch(state){
 case 'menu':startRun();break;
 case 'ready':stateT=READY_MS;break;
 case 'clear':stateT=99999;break;
 case 'over':case 'win':startRun();break;
 case 'pause':togglePause();break;}}
function togglePause(){
 if(state==='play'||state==='ready'){pausedFrom=state;state='pause';showScreen('pause');sfx.ui();}
 else if(state==='pause'){state=pausedFrom;showScreen(null);sfx.ui();}}
function toggleMute(){muted=!muted;if(master)master.gain.value=muted?0:.16;$('#btnMute').textContent=muted?'SND OFF':'SND ON';}
document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',function(){if(document.hidden&&(state==='play'||state==='ready'))togglePause();});
window.addEventListener('pointerdown',ensureAudio);

function bindBtn(id,fn){var b=$(id);b.addEventListener('click',function(){ensureAudio();sfx.ui();fn();b.blur();});}
bindBtn('#btnStart',startRun);
bindBtn('#btnRestart',startRun);
bindBtn('#btnResume',togglePause);
bindBtn('#btnRetry',startRun);
bindBtn('#btnAgain',startRun);
bindBtn('#btnMute',toggleMute);

/* ================= boot & loop ================= */
loadLevel(0);state='menu';showScreen('start');resize();updateHUD();
function loop(ts){requestAnimationFrame(loop);if(!lastTs)lastTs=ts;var dt=Math.min(50,ts-lastTs||16);lastTs=ts;T=ts;update(dt);render();}
requestAnimationFrame(loop);
})();
</script>
</body>
</html>
```

**PACOBAN** fuses the two classics: you're Pac-Man in a Sokoban maze — push wooden crates onto their glowing green docks to clear each of the 3 vaults, while chasing ghosts (a relentless chaser, an ambusher, and a will-o'-wisp) hunt you down. Munch dots for score; power pellets turn the ghosts blue so you can eat them for chained bonuses. Crates block ghost paths, so clever pushing is your best defense. Arrow keys/WASD move and push, P pauses, R retries the level, M toggles sound — all audio is synthesized live with Web Audio after your first input.
